mahākac‍cāyanasaddāpāṭha

1. sandhikappa

paṭhamakaṇḍa

(ka)

saiṭṭhaṃ  tilaukamahitaṃ abhivandiyaggaṃ,

buddhañ‍ca dhammamamalaṃ gaṇamuttamañ‍ca|

satthussa tassa vacanatthavaraṃ subuddhuṃ,

vakkhāmi suttahitamaittha susandhikappaṃ||

(kha)

saiyyaṃ jinairitanayaina budhā labhanti,

tañ‍cāpi tassa vacanatthasubaudhanaina|

atthañ‍ca akkharapadaisu amauhabhāvā,

saiyyatthikau padamatau vividhaṃ suṇaiyyaṃ||

1, 1. atthau akkharasañ‍ñātau|

sabbavacanānamatthau akkharaihaiva sañ‍ñāyatai| akkharavipattiyaṃ hi atthassa dun‍nayathā hauti, tasmā akkharakausal‍laṃ bahūpakāraṃ suttantaisu|

2, 2. akkharāpādayau  aikacattālīsaṃ|

tai ca khau akkharā api akārādayau aikacattā līsa suttantaisu saupakārā|

taṃ yathā? a ā i ī u ū ai au, ka kha ga gha ṅa, ca cha ja jha ña, ṭa ṭha ḍa ḍha ṇa, ta tha da dha na, pa pha ba bha ma, ya ra la va sa ha ḷa aṃ, iti akkharā nāma|

taina k‍vatthau? atthau akkharasañ‍ñātau|

3, 3. tatthaudantā sarā aṭṭha|

tattha akkharaisu akārādīsu audantā aṭṭha akkharā sarā nāma haunti|

taṃ yathā? a ā i ī u ū ai au, iti sarā nāma|

taina k‍vatthau? sarā sarai laupaṃ|

4, 4. lahumattā tayau rassā|

tattha aṭṭhasu saraisu lahumattā tayau sarā rassā nāma haunti|

taṃ yathā? a i u, iti rassā nāma|

taina k‍vatthau? rassaṃ|

5, 5. añ‍ñai  dīghā|

tattha aṭṭhasu saraisu rassaihi añ‍ñai pañ‍ca sarā dīghā nāma haunti|

taṃ yathā? ā ī ū ai au, iti dīghā nāma|

taina k‍vatthau? dīghaṃ|

6, 8. saisā byañ‍janā|

ṭhapaitvā aṭṭha sarai saisā akkharā kakārādayau niggahitantā byañ‍janā nāma haunti|

taṃ yathā? ka kha ga gha ṅa, ca cha ja jha ña, ṭa ṭha ḍa ḍha ṇa, ta tha da dha na, pa pha ba bha ma, ya ra la va sa ha ḷa aṃ, iti byañ‍janā nāma|

taina k‍vatthau? sarā pakati byañ‍janai|

7, 9. vaggā pañ‍capañ‍casau mantā|

taisaṃ khau byañ‍janānaṃ kakārādayau makārantā pañ‍capañ‍casau akkharavantau vaggā nāma haunti|

taṃ yathā? ka kha ga gha ṅa, ca cha ja jha ña, ṭa ṭha ḍa ḍha ṇa, ta tha da dha na, pa pha ba bha ma, iti vaggā nāma|

taina k‍vatthau? vaggantaṃ vā vaggai|

8, 10. aṃ  iti niggahitaṃ|

aṃ iti niggahitaṃ nāma hauti|

taina k‍vatthau? aṃ byañ‍janai niggahitaṃ|

9, 11. parasamañ‍ñā payaugai|

yā ca pana paraisu sak‍kataganthaisu samañ‍ñā ghausāti vā aghausāti vā, tā payaugai sati aitthāpi yuj‍jantai|

tattha ghausā nāma-ga gha ṅa, ja jha ña, ḍa ḍha ṇa, da dha na, ba bha ma, ya ra la va ha ḷa, iti ghausā nāma| aghausā nāma-ka kha, ca cha, ṭa ṭha, ta tha, pa pha, sa, iti aghausā nāma|

taina k‍vatthau? vaggai ghausāghausānaṃ tatiyapaṭhamā|

10, 12. pubbamadhauṭhita massaraṃ saraina viyaujayai|

tattha sandhiṃ kattukāmau pubbabyañ‍janaṃ adhauṭhitaṃ assaraṃ katvā sarañ‍ca upari katvā saraina viyaujayai|

tatrāyamādi|

11, 14. nayai  paraṃ yuttai|

assaraṃ khau byañ‍janaṃ adhauṭhataṃ parakkharaṃ nayai yuttai| tatrābhiratimicchaiyya|

yuttaitikasmā? ak‍kaucchi maṃ avadhi maṃ, ajini maṃ ahāsi mai| aittha pana yuttaṃ na hauti|

iti sandhikappai paṭhamau kaṇḍau|

dutiyakaṇḍa

12, 13. sarā sarai laupaṃ|

sarā khau sarai parai laupaṃ pappaunti|

yassindriyāni samathaṅgatāni| nau haitaṃ bhantai samaitāyasmā saṅghaina|

13, 15. vā parau asarūpā|

saramhā asarūpā parau sarau laupaṃ pappauti vā|

cattārau’mai bhikkhavai dhammā, kin‍nu’ māvasamaṇiyau| vāti kasmā? pañ‍cindriyāni, tayassu dhammā jahitā bhavanti|

14, 16. k‍vacāsacaṇṇaṃ  luttai|

sarau khau parau pubbasarai luttai k‍vaci asavaṇṇaṃ pappauti|

saṅkhyaṃ naupaiti vaidagū, bandhussaiva samāgamau|

k‍vacīti kasmā? yassindriyāni, tathūpamaṃ dhammavaraṃ adaisayi|

15, 17. dīghaṃ|

sarau khau parau pubbasarai luttai k‍vaci dīghaṃ pappauti| saddhīdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, anāgāraihi cūbhayaṃ|

k‍vacīti kasmā? pañ‍cahupāli aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau| natthañ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci|

16, 18. pubbau ca|

pubbau ca sarau parasaralaupai katai k‍vaci dīghaṃ pappauti|

kiṃsūdha vittaṃ purisassa saiṭṭhaṃ, sādhūti paṭissuṇitvā, k‍vacīti kasmā? itissa muhuttampi|

17, 19. yamaidantassādaisau|

aikārassa antabhūtassa sarai parai k‍vaci yakārādaisau hauti|

adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau, tyāhaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyaṃ, tyāssa pahīnā haunti|

k‍vacīti kasmā? nai’nāgatā, iti naittha|

18, 20. vamaududantānaṃ|

aukārukārānaṃ antabhūtānaṃ sarai parai k‍vaci vakārādaisau hauti|

atha khvassa, svassa hauti, bahvābādhau, vatthvaittha vihitaṃ nic‍caṃ cakkhāpāthamāgacchati|

k‍vacītikasmā? cattārau’mai bhikkhavai dhammā, kin‍numāva samaṇiyau|

19, 22. sabbau caṃti|

sabbau ic‍caisau tisaddau byañ‍janau sarai parai k‍vaci cakāraṃ pappauti|

ic‍caitaṃ  kusalaṃ, ic‍cassa vacanīyaṃ, pac‍cuttaritvā, pac‍cāharati|

k‍vacīti kasmā? itissa muhuttampi|

20, 27. dau dhassa ca|

dhaic‍caitassa sarai parai k‍vaci dakārādaisau hauti|

aikamidāhaṃ bhikkhavai samayaṃ|

k‍vacīti kasmā? idhaiva maraṇaṃ bhavissati|

vaggahaṇaina dhakārassa hakārādaisau hauti sāhu dassana mariyānaṃ|

suttavibhāgaina bahudhā siyā-

tau dassa, yathā? sugatau|

ṭau tassa, yathā? duk‍kaṭaṃ|

dhau tassa, yathā? gandhabbau|

trau ttassa, yathā? atrajau|

kau gassa, yathā? kulūpakau|

lau  rassa, yathā? mahāsālau|

jau yassa, yathā? gavajau|

bbau vassa, yathā? kubbatau|

kau yassa, yathā? sakai|

yau jassa, yathā? niyaṃputtaṃ|

kau tassa, yathā? niyakau|

c‍cau ttassa, yathā bhac‍cau|

phau passa, yathā? nipphatti|

khau kassa, yathā? nikkhamati| ic‍caivamādī yaujaitabbā|

21, 21. ivaṇṇau yaṃ navā|

pubbau ivaṇṇau sarai parai yakāraṃ pappauti navā| paṭisunthāravutyassa, sabbā vityānubhūyatai|

navāti kasmā? pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau, muttacāgī anuddhatau|

22, 28. aivādissa ri pubbau ca rassau|

saramhā parassa aivassa aikārassa ādissa rikārau hauti, pubbau ca sarau rassau hauti navā|

yathariva  vasudhātalañ‍ca sabbaṃ, tathariva guṇavā supūjaniyau|

navāti kasmā? yathā aiva, tathā aiva|

iti sandhikappai dutiyau kaṇḍau|

tatiyakaṇḍa

23, 26. sarā pakati byañ‍janai|

sarā khau byañ‍janai parai pakatirūpāni haunti|

manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā, pamādau mac‍cunau padaṃ, tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau ahu|

24, 35. sarai k‍vaci|

sarā khau sarai parai k‍vaci pakatirūpāni haunti|

kau imaṃ pathaviṃ vicaissati|

k‍vacīti kasmā? appassutāyaṃ purisau|

25, 37. dīghaṃ|

sarā khau byañ‍janai parai k‍vaci dīghaṃ pappaunti|

sammā  dhammaṃ vipassatau, aivaṃ gāmai munī carai, khantī paramaṃ tapau titikkhā|

k‍vacīti kasmā? idha maudati paic‍ca maudati, patilīyati, paṭihañ‍ñati|

26, 38. rassaṃ|

sarā khau byañ‍janai parai k‍vaci rassaṃ pappaunti|

bhauvādināma sau hauti, yathābhāvi guṇaina sau|

k‍vacīti kasmā? sammāsamādhi, sāvittī chandasau mukhaṃ, upanīyati jīvitamappamāyu|

27, 39. laupañ‍ca tatrākārau|

sarā khau byañ‍janai parai k‍vaci laupaṃ pappaunti| tatra ca laupai katai akārāgamau hauti|

sa sīlavā| sa pañ‍ñavā aisa dhammau sanantanau, sa vai kasāvamarahati, sa mānakāmaupi bhavaiyya, sa vai muni jātibhayaṃ adassi|

k‍vacīti  kasmā? sau muni, aisau dhammau padissati, na sau kāsāvamarahati|

28, 40. para dvaibhāvau ṭhānai|

saramhā parassa byañ‍janassa dvaibhāvau hauti ṭhānai|

idhappamādau, purisassa jantunau, pabbaj‍jaṃ kittayissāmi, cātuddasi, pañ‍caddasi, abhik‍kantatarau candau|

ṭhānaiti kasmā? idha maudati paic‍ca maudati|

29, 42. vaggai ghausāghausānaṃ tatiyapaṭhamā|

vaggai khau pubbaisaṃ byañ‍janānaṃ ghausāghausabhūtānaṃ saramhā yathāsaṅkhyaṃ tatiyapaṭhamakkharā dvaibhāvaṃ gacchanti ṭhānai|

aisaiva cajjhānapphalau, yatraṭṭhitaṃ nappasahaiyya mac‍cu, sailai yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau, cattāriṭṭhānāni narau pamattau|

ṭhānaiti kasmā? idha caitasau daḷhaṃ gaṇhāti thāmasā|

iti sandhikappai tatiyau kaṇḍau|

catutthakaṇḍa

30, 58. aṃ  byañ‍janai niggahitaṃ|

niggahitaṃ khau byañ‍janai parai aṃ iti hauti|

aivaṃ vuttai, taṃ sādhūti paṭissuṇitvā|

31, 49. vaggantaṃ vā vaggai|

vaggabhūtai byañ‍janai parai niggahitaṃ khau vaggantaṃ vā pappauti|

tan‍nic‍cutaṃ, dhammañ‍carai sucaritaṃ, cirappavāsiṃ purisaṃ| santantassa manaṃ hauti, taṅkāruṇikaṃ, aivaṅkhau bhikkhavai sikkhitabbaṃ|

vāggahaṇainaniggahitaṃ khau lakārādaisau hauti| puggalaṃ|

vāti kasmā? na taṃ kammaṃ kataṃ sādhu|

32, 50. aihai uṃ|

aikārahakārai parai niggahitaṃ khau ñakāraṃ pappauti vā|

pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyissāmi, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi, aivañhi vau bhikkhavai sikkhitabbaṃ| tañhi tassa musā hauti|

vāti  kasmā? aivamaitaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ hauti subhāsitaṃ|

33, 50. sayai ca|

niggahitaṃ khau yakārai parai saha yakāraina ñakāraṃ pappauti vā|

sañ‍ñaugau, sañ‍ñuttaṃ|

vāti kasmā? saṃyaugau, saṃyuttaṃ|

34, 52. madā sarai|

niggahitassa khau sarai parai makāradakārādaisā haunti vā|

tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇaṃ, aitadavauca satthā|

vāti kasmā? ak‍kaucchi maṃ avadhi maṃ, ajini maṃ ahāsi mai|

35, 34. ya va ma da na ta ra lā cāgamā|

sarai parai yakārau vakārau makārau dakārau nakārau takārau rakārau lakārau imai āgamā haunti vā|

nayimassa vij‍jā, yathayidaṃ cittaṃ| migī bhantā vudikkhati, sittā tai lahu maissati, asittā tai garu maissati| assau  bhadrau kasāmiva, sammadañ‍ñā vimuttānaṃ| manasādañ‍ñā vimuttānaṃ, attadatthamabhiñ‍ñāya| ciraṃnāyati, itau nāyati| yasmātiha bhikkhavai, tasmātiha bhikkhavai, aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ| sabbhiraiva samāsaitha, āraggairiva sāsapau, sāsapauriva āraggā| chaḷabhiñ‍ñā, saḷāyatanaṃ|

vāti kasmā? aivaṃ mahiddhiyā aisā, ak‍kaucchi maṃ avadhi maṃ, ajini maṃ ahāsi mai, ajaiyyau anugāmikau|

caggahaṇaina idhaiva makārassa pakārau hauti| cirappavāsiṃ purisaṃ|

kakārassa ca dakārau hauti| sadatthapasutau siyā|

dakārassa ca takārau hauti, sugatau|

36, 47. k‍vaci au byañ‍janai|

byañ‍janai parai k‍vaci aukārāgamau hauti|

atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ| parausahassaṃ|

k‍vacīki  kasmā? aitha passathimaṃ laukaṃ, andhībhūtau ayaṃ laukau|

37, 57. niggahitañ‍ca|

niggahitañ‍cāgamau hauti sarai vā byañ‍janai vā parai k‍vaci|

cakkhuṃudapādi, avaṃsirau, yāvañ‍cidha bhikkhavai purimaṃ jātiṃ sarāmi, aṇuṃthūlāni sabbasau, manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā|

k‍vacīti kasmā? idhaiva naṃ pasaṃsanti, paic‍ca saggai pamaudati, na hi aitaihi yānaihi, gacchaiyya agataṃ disaṃ|

caggahaṇaina visaddassa ca pakārau hauti| pacaissati, vicaissati vā|

38, 53. k‍vaci laupaṃ|

niggahitaṃ khau sarai parai k‍vaci laupaṃ pappauti|

tāsāhaṃ santikai, vidūnaggamiti|

k‍vacīti kasmā? ahamaiva nūna bālau aitamatthaṃ viditvāna|

39, 54. byañ‍janai  ca|

niggahitaṃ khau byañ‍janai parai k‍vaci laupaṃ pappauti|

ariyasac‍cānadassanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhānasāsanaṃ|

k‍vacīti kasmā? aitaṃ maṅgalamuttamaṃ, taṃ vau vadāmi bhaddantai|

40, 55. parau vāsarau|

niggahitamhā parau sarau laupaṃ pappauti vā|

bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti, uttattaṃva, yathābījaṃva, yathādhañ‍ñaṃva|

vāti kasmā? ahamaiva nūna bālau, aitadahausi|

41, 56. byañ‍janau ca visañ‍ñaugau|

niggahitamhā parasmiṃ sarai luttai yadi byañ‍janau sasañ‍ñaugau visañ‍ñaugau hauti|

aivaṃsa tai āsavau, pupphaṃsā uppaj‍ji|

luttaiti kasmā? aivamassa vidūnaggamiti|

caggahaṇaina  tiṇṇaṃ byañ‍janānamantarai yai sarūpā, taisampi laupau hauti| agyāgāraṃ, paṭisanthāravutyassa|

iti sandhikappai catutthau kaṇḍau|

pañ‍camakaṇḍa

42, 32. gausarai puthassāgamau k‍vaci|

puthaic‍caitassa antai sarai parai k‍vaci gakārāgamau hauti|

puthagai va|

43, 33. pāssa cantau rassau|

pāic‍caitassa antai sarai parai k‍vaci gakārāgamau hauti, antau ca sarau rassau hauti|

pagaiva vutyassa|

44, 24. abbhau abhi|

45, 25. ajjhau  adhi|

ajjhaukāsai, ajjhāgamā|

46, 26. tai na vā ivaṇṇai|

tai ca khau abhiadhiic‍caitai ivaṇṇai parai abbhau ajjhauitivuttarūpā naṃ haunti vā|

abhicchitaṃ, adhīritaṃ|

47, 23. atissa cantassa|

atiic‍caitassa antabhūtassa tisaddassa ivaṇṇai parai ‘‘sabbau caṃ tī’’ti vuttarūpaṃ na hauti|

atīsigaṇau, atīritaṃ|

48, 43. k‍vaci paṭi patissa|

patiic‍caitassa sarai vā byañ‍janai vā parai k‍vaci paṭiādaisau hauti|

paṭaggi dātabbau, paṭihañ‍ñati|

49, 44. puthassu byañ‍janai|

puthuj‍janau, puthubhūtaṃ|

antaggahaṇaina aputhassāpi sarai parai antassa ukārau hauti, manuñ‍ñaṃ|

50, 45. au avassa|

avaic‍caitassa byañ‍janai parai k‍vaci aukārau hauti|

andhakāraina aunaddhā|

k‍vacīti kasmā? avasussatu mai sarīrai maṃsalauhitaṃ|

51, 59. anupadiṭṭhānaṃ vuttayaugatau|

anupadiṭṭhānaṃ upasagganipātānaṃ sarasandhīhi byañ‍janasandhīhi vuttasandhīhi ca yathāyaugaṃ yaujaitabbaṃ|

pāpanaṃ, parāyaṇaṃ, upāyanaṃ, upāhanaṃ, nyāyaugau, nigupadhi, anubaudhau, duvūpasantaṃ, suvūpasantaṃ, dvālayau, svālayau, durākhyātaṃ, svākhyātau, udīritaṃ, samuddiṭṭhaṃ, viyaggaṃ, vijjhaggaṃ, byaggaṃ, avayāgamanaṃ, anvaiti, anupaghātau, anacchariyaṃ, pariyaisanā, parāmāsau, aivaṃ sarai ca haunti|

pariggahau, paggahau, pak‍kamau, parak‍kamau, nik‍kamau, nik‍kasāvau, nil‍layanaṃ, dul‍layanaṃ, dumbhikkhaṃ, dubbuttaṃ, sandiṭṭhaṃ, duggahau, viggahau, niggatau, abhik‍kamau, paṭik‍kamau, aivaṃ byañ‍janai ca| saisā sabbai yaujaitabbā|

2. nāmakappa

paṭhamakaṇḍa

52, 60. jinavacanayuttaṃ  hi|

‘‘jinavacanayuttaṃ hi’’ ic‍caitaṃ adhikāratthaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

53, 61. liṅgañ‍ca nippaj‍jatai|

yathā yathā jinavacanayuttaṃ hi liṅgaṃ, tathā tathā idha liṅgañ‍ca nippaj‍jatai|

taṃ yathā? aisau nau satthā, brahmā attā, sakhā, rājā|

54, 62. tatau ca vibhattiyau|

tatau jinavacanayuttaihi liṅgaihi vibhattiyau parā haunti|

55, 63. si yau, aṃ yau, nā hi, sa naṃ, smā hi, sa naṃ, smiṃ su|

kā ca pana tāyau vibhattiyau? si, yau iti paṭhamā, aṃ, yauiti dutiyā, nā hi iti tatiyā, sa, naṃiti catutthī, smā, hi iti pañ‍camī, sa, naṃ iti chaṭṭhī, smiṃ, su iti sattamī|

vibhattiic‍canaina  k‍vatthau? amhassa mamaṃ savibhattissa sai|

56, 64. tadanuparaudhaina|

yathā yathā taisaṃ jinavacanānaṃ anuparaudhau| tathā tathā idha liṅgañ‍ca nippaj‍jatai|

57, 71. ālapanai si ga sañ‍ñau|

ālapanatthai si gasañ‍ñau hauti|

bhauti ayyai, bhauti kañ‍ñai, bhauti kharādiyai|

ālapanaiti kimatthaṃ? sā ayyā|

sīti kimatthaṃ? bhautiyau ayyāyau|

gaic‍canaina k‍vatthau? ghatai ca|

58, 29. ivaṇṇuvaṇṇā jhalā|

ivaṇṇuvaṇṇāic‍caitai jhalasañ‍ñā haunti yathāsaṅkhyaṃ|

isinau, agginau, gahapatinau, daṇḍinau| saitunau, kaitunau, bhikkhunau| sayambhunau, abhibhunau|

jhalaic‍canaina k‍vatthau? jhalatau sassa nau vā|

59, 182. tai itthikhyā pau|

tai ivaṇṇuvaṇṇā yadā itthikhyā, tadā pasañ‍ñā haunti|

rattiyā, itthiyā, dhainuyā, vadhuyā|

itthikhyāti kimatthaṃ? isinā, bhikkhunā|

saic‍canaina  k‍vatthau? patau yā|

60, 177. ā ghau|

ākārau yadā itthikhyau, tadā ghasañ‍ñau hauti|

saddhāya, kañ‍ñāya, vīṇāya, gaṅgāya, disāya sālāya, mālāya, tulāya, daulāya, pabhāya, saubhāya, pañ‍ñāya, karuṇāya nāvāya, kapālikāya|

āti kimatthaṃ? rattiyā, itthiyā|

itthikhyauti kimatthaṃ? satthārā daisitau ayaṃ dhammau|

ghaic‍canaina k‍vatthau? ghatau nādīnaṃ|

61, 86. sāgamau sai|

sakārāgamau hauti sai vibhattimhi|

purisassa, aggissa, isissa, daṇḍissa, bhikkhussa, sayambhussa, abhibhussa|

saiti kimattaṃ? purisasmiṃ|

62, 206. saṃsāsvaikavacanaisu ca|

saṃsāsu aikavacanaisu vibhattādaisaisu sakārāgamau hauti|

aitissaṃ, aitissā imissaṃ, imissā, tissaṃ, tissā,

tassaṃ tassā, yassaṃ, yassā, amussaṃ, amussā|

saṃsāsvīti kimatthaṃ? agginā, pāṇinā|

aikavacanaisvīti kimatthaṃ? tāsaṃ, sabbāsaṃ|

vibhattādaisaisvīti kimatthaṃ? manasā, vacasā, thāmasā|

63, 217. aitimāsami|

aitāimāic‍caitaisamantau sarau ikārau hauti saṃsāsu aikavacanaisu vibhattādaisaisu|

aitissaṃ, aitissā, imissaṃ, imissā|

saṃsāsvīti  kimatthaṃ? aitāya, imāya|

aikavacanaisvīti kimatthaṃ? aitāsaṃ, imāsaṃ|

64, 216. tassā vā|

tassā itthiyaṃ vattamānassa antassa ākārassa ikārau hauti vā saṃsāsu aikavacanaisu vibhattādaisaisu|

tissaṃ, tissā, tassaṃ, tassā|

65, 215. tatau sassa ssāya|

tatau tā aitā imātau sassa vibhattissa ssāyādaisau hauti vā|

tissāya, aitissāya, imissāya|

vāti kimatthaṃ? tissā, aitissā, imissā|

66, 205. ghau rassaṃ|

ghau rassamāpaj‍jatai saṃsāsu aikavacanaisu vibhattādaisaisu|

tassaṃ, tassā, yassaṃ, yassā, sabbassaṃ, sabbassā|

saṃsāsvīti kimatthaṃ? tāya, sabbāya|

aikavacanaisvīti kimatthaṃ? tāsaṃ, sabbāsaṃ|

67, 229. nau  ca dvāditau naṃmhi|

dviic‍caivamāditau saṅkhyātau nakārāgamau hauti naṃmhi vibhattimhi|

dvin‍naṃ, tin‍naṃ, catun‍naṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ, chan‍naṃ, sattan‍naṃ, aṭṭhan‍naṃ, navan‍naṃ, dasan‍naṃ|

dvāditauti kimatthaṃ? sahassānaṃ|

naṃmhīti kimatthaṃ? dvīsu, tīsu|

caggahaṇainassañ‍cāgamau hauti| catassan‍naṃ itthīnaṃ tissan‍naṃ vaidanānaṃ|

68, 184. amā patau smiṃsmānaṃ vā|

paic‍caitasmā smiṃsmāic‍caitaisaṃ aṃāādaisā haunti vā yathāsaṅkhyaṃ|

matyaṃ, matiyaṃ, matyā, matiyā, nikatyaṃ| nikatiyaṃ, nikatyā, nikatiyā, vikatyaṃ, vikatiyaṃ, vikatyā, vikatiyā, viratyaṃ, viratiyaṃ, viratyā, viratiyā, ratyaṃ, ratiyaṃ, ratyā, ratiyā, puthabyaṃ, puthaviyaṃ, puthabyā, puthaviyā, pavatyaṃ, pavatyā, pavattiyaṃ, pavattiyā|

69, 186. āditau au ca|

ādiic‍caitasmā smiṃvacanassa aṃauādaisā haunti vā|

ādīṃ, ādau|

vāti kimatthaṃ? ādismiṃ, ādimhi nāthaṃ namassitvāna,

caggahaṇaina añ‍ñasmāpi smiṃ vacanassa ā au aṃādaisā haunti| divā ca rattau ca haranti yai bali| bārāṇasiṃ ahu rājā|

70, 30. jhalānamiyuvā sarai vā|

jhalaic‍caitaisaṃ iya uvaic‍caitai ādaisā haunti vā sarai parai yathāsaṅkhyaṃ|

tiyantaṃ pacchiyāgārai, aggiyāgārai, bhikkhuvāsanai nisīdati, vuthuvāsanai nisīdati|

saraiti kimatthaṃ? timalaṃ, tiphalaṃ, ticatuk‍kaṃ, tidaṇḍaṃ, tilaukaṃ, tinayanaṃ, tipāsaṃ, tihaṃsaṃ, tibhavaṃ, tikhandhaṃ, tipiṭakaṃ, tivaidanaṃ, catuddisaṃ, puthubhūtaṃ|

vāti kimatthaṃ? pañ‍cahaṅgaihi tīhākāraihi| cakkhāyatanaṃ|

vāti vikappanatthaṃ, ikārassa ayādaisau hauti, vatthuttayaṃ|

71, 505. yavakārā ca|

jhalānaṃ yakāra vakārādaisā haunti sarai parai yathāsaṅkhyaṃ|

agyāgāraṃ, pakkhāyatanaṃ, svāgataṃ, tai mahāvīra|

262, 391. vuḍḍhassa jau iyiṭṭhaisu|

269, 401. yavataṃ ta la ṇa dakārānaṃ byañ‍janāni ca la ña jakārattaṃ|

3. kārakakappa

chaṭṭhakaṇḍa

271, 88, 308. yasmā  dapaiti bhayamādattai vā tadapādānaṃ|

yasmā vā apaiti, yasmā vā bhayaṃ jāyatai, yasmā vā ādattai, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

taṃ yathā? gāmā apainti munayau, nagarā niggatau rājā, caurā bhayaṃ jāyatai, ācariyupajjhāyaihi sikkhaṃ gaṇhāti sissau|

apādānamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? apādānai pañ‍camī|

272, 309. dhātunā mānamupasaggayaugādvīsvapi ca|

dhātunāmānaṃ payaugai ca upasaggayaugādīsvapi ca taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

dhātūnaṃ payaugai tāva jiic‍caitassa dhātussa parāpubbassa payaugai yau asahau, sau apādānasañ‍ñau hauti|

taṃ yathā? buddhasmā parājainti añ‍ñatitthiyā|

bhūic‍caitassa  dhātussa papubbassa payaugai yatau acchin‍nappabhavau, sau apādānasañ‍ñau hauti|

taṃ yathā? himavatā pabhavanti pañ‍ca mahānadiyau, anavatattamhā pabhavanti mahāsarā, aciravatiyā pabhavanti kun‍nadiyau|

nāmappayaugaipi taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

taṃ yathā? urasmā jātau puttau, bhūmitau niggatau rasau, ubhatau sujātau puttau mātitau ca pititau ca|

upasaggayaugai taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

taṃ yathā? apasālāya āyanti vāṇijā, ābrahmalaukā saddau abbhuggacchati upari pabbatā daivau vassati, buddhasmā pati sāriputtau dhammadaisanāya bhikkhū ālapati taimāsaṃ, ghatamassa tailasmā pati dadāti, uppalamassa padumasmā pati dadāti, kanakamassa hirañ‍ñasmā pati dadāti|

ādiggahaṇaina kārakamajjhaipi pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti| itau pakkhasmā vijjhati migaṃ luddakau, kausā vijjhati kuñ‍jaraṃ, māsasmā bhuñ‍jati bhaujanaṃ|

apiggahaṇaina nipātapayaugaipi pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti dutiyā ca tatiyā ca| rahitā mātujā puñ‍ñaṃ katvā  dānaṃ daiti, rahitā mātujaṃ, rahitā mātujaina vā| ritai saddhammā kutau sukhaṃ labhati, ritai saddhammaṃ, ritai saddhammaina vā| tai bhikkhū nānā kulā pabbajitā, vinā saddhammā natthañ‍ñau kauci nāthau laukai vij‍jati, vinā saddhammaṃ, vinā saddhammaina vā| vinā buddhasmā, vinā buddhaṃ, vinā buddhaina vā|

caggahaṇaina añ‍ñatthāpi pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti| yatauhaṃ bhagini ariyāya jātiyā jātau| yatau sarāmi attānaṃ, yatau pattausmi viñ‍ñutaṃ, yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāsavaiyyuṃ|

273, 310. rakkhaṇatthānamicchitaṃ|

rakkhaṇatthānaṃ dhātūnaṃ payaugai yaṃ icchitaṃ, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

kākai rakkhanti taṇḍulā, yavā paṭisaidhainti gāvau|

274, 311. yaina vā’ dassanaṃ|

yaina vā adassanamicchitaṃ, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

upajjhāyā antaradhāyati sissau, mātarā ca pitarā ca antaradhāyati puttau|

vāti  kimatthaṃ? sattamīvibhatyattaṃ| jaitavanai antaradhāyati bhagavā|

275, 312. dūranti kaddha kāla nimmāna tvālaupa disāyaugavibhattārappayauga suddhappamaucana haitu vivittappamāṇa pubbayaugabandhana guṇavacana pañha kathana thaukākattūsu ca|

dūratthai, antikatthai, addhanimmānai, kālanimmānai, tvālaupai, disāyaugai, vibhattai, ārappayaugai, suddhai, pamaucanai, haitvatthai, vivittatthai, pamāṇai, pubbayaugai, bandhanatthai, guṇavacanai, pañhai, kathanai, thaukai, akattari ca ic‍caitaisvatthaisu, payaugaisu ca, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

dūratthaitāva – kīva dūrau itau naḷakāragāmau, dūratau vā gamma, ārakā tai maughapurisā imasmā dhammavinayā| dutiyā ca tatiyā ca, dūraṃ gāmaṃ āvatau, dūraina gāmaina vā āgatau| ārakā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, anaina dhammavinayaina vā ic‍caivamādi|

antikatthai – antikaṃ gāmā, āsan‍naṃ gāmā, samīpaṃ gāmā, samīpaṃ saddhammā| dutiyā ca tatiyā ca, antikaṃ gāmaṃ, antikaṃ gāmaina vā| āsan‍naṃ gāmaṃ, āsan‍naṃ gāmaina vā| samīpaṃ gāmaṃ| samīpaṃ gāmaina vā| samīpaṃ saddhammaṃ, samīpaṃ saddhammaina vā ic‍caivamādi|

addhanimmānai  – itau mathurāya catūsu yaujanaisu saṅkassaṃ nāma nagaraṃ atthi, tattha bahū janā vasanti ic‍caivamādi|

kālanimmānai – itau bhikkhavai aikanavutikappai vipassī nāma bhagavā laukai udapādi, itau tiṇṇaṃ māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina parinibbāyissati ic‍caivamādi|

tvālaupai kammādhikaraṇaisu – pāsādā saṅkamaiyya, pāsādaṃ abhiruhitvā vā| pabbatā saṅkamaiyya, pabbataṃ abhiruhitvā vā| hatthikkhandhā saṅkamaiyya, hatthikkhandhaṃ abhiruhitvā vā| āsanā vuṭṭhahaiyya| āsanai nisīditvā vā ic‍caivamādi|

disāyaugai – avicitau yāva uparibhavaggamantarai bahū sattanikāyā vasanti, yatau khaimaṃ tatau bhayaṃ, puratthimatau, dakkhiṇatau, pacchimatau, uttaratau aggī paj‍jalanti, yatau assausuṃ bhagavantaṃ, uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā ic‍caivamādi|

vibhattai – yatau paṇītatarau vā visiṭṭhatarau vā natthi| chaṭṭhī ca, chan‍navutīnaṃ pāsaṇḍānaṃ, dhammānaṃ pavaraṃ yadidaṃ sugatavinayau ic‍caivamādi|

ārappayaugai –  gāmadhammā vasaladhammā asaddhammā ārati virati paṭivirati, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī ic‍caivamādi|

suddhai – laubhaniyaihi dhammaihi suddhau asaṃsaṭṭhau, mātitau ca pititau ca suddhau asaṃsaṭṭhau anupakuddhau agarahitau ic‍caivamādi|

pamaucanai – parimuttau dukkhasmāti vadāmi, muttausmi mārabandhanā, na tai muc‍canti mac‍cunā ic‍caivamādi|

haitvatthai – kasmā haitunā, kaina haitunā, kissa haitunā, kasmā nu tumhaṃ daharā na mīyarai, kasmā idhaiva maraṇaṃ bhavissati ic‍caivamādi|

vivittatthai – vivittau pāpakā dhammā, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi ic‍caivamādi|

pamāṇai – dīghasau navavidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā pamāṇikā kāraitabbā, majjhimassa purisassa aḍḍha taiḷasahatthā ic‍caivamādi|

pubbayaugai – pubbaiva sammaudhā ic‍caivamādi|

bandhanatthai – satasmā bandhau narau| tatiyā ca, sataina bandhau narau rañ‍ñā iṇatthaina ic‍caivamādi|

guṇavacanai  – puñ‍ñāya sugatiṃ yanti, cāgāya vipulaṃ dhanaṃ, pañ‍ñāya vimuttimanau, issariyāya janaṃ rakkhati rājā ic‍caivamādi|

pañhai tvālaupai kammādhikaraṇaisu – abhidhammā pucchanti, abhidhammaṃ sutvā, abhidhammai ṭhatvā vā| vinayā pucchanti, vinayaṃ sutvā, vinayai ṭhatvā vā| dutiyā ca tatiyā ca, abhidhammaṃ, abhidhammaina vā| vinayaṃ, vinayaina vā| aiva suttā, gaiyyā, gāthāya, vaiyyākaraṇā, udānā, itivuttakā, jātakā, abbhutadhammā, vaidal‍lā ic‍caivamādi|

kathanai tvālaupai kammādhikaraṇaisu – abhidhammā kathayanti, abhidhammaṃ sutvā, abhidhammai ṭhatvā vā| vinayā kathayanti, vinayaṃ sutvā, vinayai ṭhatvā vā| dutiyā ca tatiyā ca, abhidhammaṃ, abhidhammaina vā| vinayaṃ vinayaina vā| aivaṃ suttā, gaiyyā, gāthāya, vaiyyākaraṇā, udānā, itivuttakā, jātakā, abbhutadhammā, vaidal‍lā ic‍caivamādi|

thaukai – thaukā muc‍canti| appamattakā muc‍canti, kicchā muc‍canti| tatiyā ca| thaukaina, appamattakaina, kicchaina vā ic‍caivamādi|

akattarica – kammassa katattā upacitattā ussan‍nattā vipulattā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti ic‍caivamādi|

caggahaṇaina  saisaisupi yai mayā naupadiṭṭhā apādānapayaugikā, tai payaugavicakkhaṇaihi yathāyaugaṃ yaujaitabbā|

276, 302. yassa dātukāmau raucatai dhārayatai vā taṃ sampadānaṃ|

yassa vā dātukāmau yassa vāraucatai, yassa vā dhārayatai, taṃ kārakaṃ sampadānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

samaṇassa cīvaraṃ dadāti, samaṇassa raucatai sac‍caṃ, daivadattassa suvaṇṇacchattaṃ dhārayatai yañ‍ñadattau|

sampadānamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? sampadānai catutthī|

vāti vikappanatthaṃ, dhātunāmānaṃ payaugai vā upasaggappayaugai vā nipātappayaugai vā sati atthavikappanatthaṃ vāti padaṃ payuj‍jati|

277, 303. silāgha hanu ṭhā sapa dhāra piha kudha duhissausūya rādhikkha pac‍cāsuṇa anupatigiṇa pubbakattāraucanattha tadattha tumatthālamattha mañ‍ñānādarappāṇini, gatyatthakammani, āsīsattha sammuti bhiyya sattamyatthaisu ca|

silāgha hanu ṭhā sapa dhāra piha kudha duha issaic‍caitaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ payaugai, usūyatthānañ‍ca payaugai, rādhikkhappayaugai, pac‍cāsuṇaanupatigiṇānaṃ pubbakattari, āraucanatthai, tadatthai, tumatthai, alamatthai, mañ‍ñatippayaugai anādarai appāṇini, gatyatthānaṃ dhātūnaṃ kammani, āsīsatthai ca sammuti bhiyya sattamyatthaisu ca, taṃ kārakaṃ sampadānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

silāghappayaugai tāva – buddhassa silāghatai, dhammassa silāghatai, saṅghassa silāghatai, sakaṃupajjhāyassa silāghatai, tava silāghatai mama silāghatai ic‍caivamādi|

hanuppayaugai – hanutai tuyhamaiva, hanutai mayhamaiva ic‍caivamādi|

ṭhāpayaugai – upatiṭṭhaiyya sakyaputtānaṃ vaḍḍhakī, bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jantassa pānīyaina vā vidhūpanaina vā upatiṭṭhaiyya bhikkhunī ic‍caivamādi|

sapappayaugai – tuyhaṃ sapatai, mayhaṃ sapatai ic‍caivamādi|

dhārappayaugai – suvaṇṇaṃ tai dhārayatai ic‍caivamādi|

pihappayaugai – buddhassa añ‍ñatitthiyā pihayanti, daivā dassanakāmā tai, yatau icchāmi bhaddantassa, samiddhānaṃ pihayanti daliddā ic‍caivamādi|

kudhaduhaissausūyappayaugai – kaudhayati daiva dattassa, tassa kujjha mahāvīra, mā raṭṭhaṃ vinassa idaṃ| duhayati  disānaṃ maighau, titthiyā samaṇānaṃ issayanti guṇagiddhaina, titthiyā samaṇānaṃ issayanti lābhagiddhaina, duj‍janā guṇavantānaṃ usūyanti guṇagiddhaina, kā usūyā vijānataṃ ic‍caivamādi|

rādha ikkha ic‍caitaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ payaugai yassa akathitassa pucchanaṃ kammavikkhyāpanatthañ‍ca, taṃ kārakaṃ sampadānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti, dutiyā ca|

ārādhauhaṃ rañ‍ñau, ārādhauhaṃ rājānaṃ, kyāhaṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi, kyāhaṃ ayyai aparajjhāmi, cakkhuṃ janassa dassanāya taṃ viya mañ‍ñai, āyasmatau upālitthairassa upasampadāpaikkhau upatissau, āyasmantaṃ vā ic‍caivamādi|

pac‍cāsuṇa anupatigiṇānaṃ pubbakattari suṇautissa pac‍cāyaugai yassa kammunau pubbassa yau kattā, sau sampadānasañ‍ñau hauti|

taṃ yathā? bhagavā bhikkhū aitadavauca|

bhikkhūti akathīta kammaṃ, aitanti kathitakammaṃ| yassa kammunau pubbassa yau kattā, sau‘bhagavā’ti ‘‘yau karauti sa kattā’’ti suttavacanaina kattusañ‍ñau| aivaṃ yassa  kammunau pubbassa yau kattā, sau sampadānasañ‍ñau hauti|

taṃ yathā? tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ, āsuṇanti buddhassa bhikkhū|

giṇassa anupatiyaugai yassa kammunau pubbassa yau kattā, sau sampadānasañ‍ñau hauti|

taṃ yathā? bhikkhu janaṃ dhammaṃ sāvaiti, tassa bhikkhunau janau anugiṇāti, tassa bhikkhunau janau patigiṇāti|

yau vadaiti sa‘kattā’ti,

vuttaṃ ‘kamma’nti vuc‍cati|

yau paṭiggāhakau tassa,

‘sampadānaṃ’ vijāniyā||

ic‍caivamādi|

āraucanatthai – āraucayāmi vau bhikkhavai, āmantayāmi vau bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau bhikkhavai, āraucayāmi tai mahārāja, āmantayāmi tai mahārāja, paṭivaidayāmi tai mahārāja ic‍caivamādi|

tadatthai – ūnassa pāripūriyā taṃ cīvaraṃ nikkhipitabbaṃ| buddhassa atthāya, dhammassa atthāya, saṅghassa atthāya, jīvitaṃ paric‍cajāmi ic‍caivamādi|

tumatthai  -laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ buddhau laukai uppaj‍jati| bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya vinayau pañ‍ñattau ic‍caivamādi|

alamatthappayaugai-alamiti arahati paṭikkhittaisu| alaṃ mai buddhau, alaṃ mai raj‍jaṃ, alaṃ bhikkhu pattassa, alaṃ mal‍lau mal‍lassa, arahati mal‍lau mal‍lassa| paṭikkhittai alaṃ tai rūpaṃ karaṇīyaṃ, alaṃ mai hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaina ic‍caivamādi|

mañ‍ñatippayaugai anādarai appāṇini-kaṭṭhassa tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai, kaliṅgarassa tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai|

anādaraiti kimatthaṃ? suvaṇṇaṃ viya taṃ mañ‍ñai|

appāṇinīti kimatthaṃ? gadrabhaṃ tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai ic‍caivamādi|

gatyatthakammani-gāmassa pādaina gatau, nagarassa pādaina gatau, appau saggāya gacchati, saggassa gamanaina vā, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya saṅghau| dutiyā ca, gāmaṃ pādaina gatau, nagaraṃ pādaina gatau, appau saggaṃ gacchati, saggaṃ gamanaina vā, mūlaṃ paṭikassaiyya saṅghau ic‍caivamādi|

āsīsatthai-āyasmatau dīghāyukau hautu, bhaddaṃ bhavatau hautu, kusalaṃ bhavatau hautu, anāmayaṃ  bhavatau hautu, sukhaṃ bhavatau hautu, svāgataṃ bhavatau hautu, atthau bhavatau hautu, hitaṃ bhavatau hautu ic‍caivamādi|

sammutippayaugai– añ‍ñatra saṅghasammutiyā bhikkhussa vippavatthuṃ na vaṭṭati, sādhu sammuti mai tassa bhagavatau dassanāya ic‍caivamādi|

bhiyyappayaugai – bhiyyausau mattāyaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

sattamyatthai – tuyhañ‍cassa āvi karaumi, tassa mai sak‍kau pāturahausi ic‍caivamādi|

atthaggahaṇaina bahūsu akkharappayaugaisu dissati|

taṃ yathā? upamaṃ mata karissāmi, dhammaṃ vau daisaissāmi|

sāratthai ca – daisaitu bhantai bhagavā dhammaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ| tassa phāsu vihārāya hauti, aitassa pahiṇaiyya, yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya, tathāpi taisaṃ byākarissāma, kappati samaṇānaṃ āyaugau, amhākaṃ maṇinā atthau, kimatthau mai buddhaina, saiyyau mai atthau, bahūpakārā bhantai mahāpajā patigautamī bhagavatau, bahūpakārā bhikkhavai mātāpitarau puttānaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

saisaisu akkharappayaugaisupi añ‍ñaipi payaugā payaugavicakkhaṇaihi yaujaitabbā|

caggahaṇaṃ vikappanatthavāggahaṇānukaḍḍhanatthaṃ| yai kaici saddā sampadānappayaugikā mayā naupadiṭṭhā, taisaṃ gahaṇatthaṃ idha vikappīyati vāsaddau|

taṃ yathā? bhikkhusaṅghassa pabhū ayaṃ bhagavā, daisassa pabhū ayaṃ rājā| khaittassa pabhū ayaṃ gahapati, arañ‍ñassa pabhū ayaṃ luddakau ic‍caivamādi| k‍vaci dutiyā tatiyā pañ‍camī chaṭṭhī sattamyatthaisu ca|

178, 320. yaudhārau tamaukāsaṃ|

yau ādhārau, taṃ aukāsasañ‍ñaṃ hauti| svādhārau catubbidhau byāpikau, aupasilaisikau, vaisayikau sāmīpikau cāti|

tattha  byāpikau tāva–jalaisu khīraṃ tiṭṭhati, tilaisu tailaṃ, ucchūsu rasau|

aupasilaisikau–pariyaṅkai rājā saiti, āsanai upaviṭṭhau saṅghau|

vaisayikau–bhūmīsu manussā caranti, antalikkhai vāyū vāyanti| ākāsai sakuṇā pakkhandanti|

sāmīpikau–vanai hatthinau caranti, gaṅgāyaṃ ghausau tiṭṭhati, vajai gāvau duhanti, sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai|

aukāsamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? aukāsai sattamī|

279, 292. yaina vā kayiratai taṃ karaṇaṃ|

yaina vā kayiratai, yaina vā passati, yaina vā suṇāti, taṃ kārakaṃ karaṇasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

dattaina vihiṃ lunāti, vāsiyā kaṭṭhaṃ tacchati, pharasunā rukkhaṃ chindati, kudālaina pathaviṃ khaṇati, satthaina kammaṃ karauti| cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati|

karaṇamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? karaṇai tatiyā|

280, 285. yaṃ  karauti taṃ kammaṃ|

yaṃ vā karauti, yaṃ vā passati, yaṃ vā suṇāti, taṃ kārakaṃ kammasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

chattaṃ karauti, rathaṃ karauti, rūpaṃ passati, saddaṃ suṇāti, kaṇṭakaṃ maddati, visaṃ gilati|

kammamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? kammatthai dutiyā|

281, 294. yau karauti sa kattā|

yau karauti, sau kattusañ‍ñau hauti|

ahinā daṭṭhau narau, garuḷaina hatau nāgau| buddhaina jitau mārau, upaguttaina mārau bandhau|

kattuic‍canaina k‍vatthau? kattari ca|

282, 295. yau kāraiti sa haitu|

yau kattāraṃ kāraiti, sau haitusañ‍ñau hauti, kattā ca|

sau purisau taṃ purisaṃ kammaṃ kāraiti sau purisau taina purisaina kammaṃ kāraiti, sau purisau tassa purisassa kammaṃ kāraiti| aivaṃ hāraiti pāṭhaiti pācaiti, dhāraiti|

haituic‍canaina k‍vatthau? dhātūhi ṇai ṇaya ṇāpai ṇāpayā kāritāni haitvatthai|

283, 316. yassa  vā pariggahau taṃ sāmī|

yassa vā pariggahau, taṃ sāmīsañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

tassa bhikkhunau paṭivīsau, tassa bhikkhunau pattau, tassa bhikkhunau cīvaraṃ, attanau mukhaṃ|

sāmīic‍canaina k‍vatthau? sāmismiṃ chaṭṭhī|

284, 283. liṅgatthai paṭhamā|

liṅgatthābhidhānamattai paṭhamāvibhatti hauti|

purisau, purisā, aikau, dvai, ca, vā, hai, ahai, rai, arai|

285, 70. ālapanai ca|

ālapanatthā dhikai liṅgatthābhidhānamattai ca paṭhamāvibhatti hauti|

bhau purisa, bhavantau purisā, bhau rāja, bhavantau rājānau, hai sakhai, hai sakhinau|

286, 291. karaṇai tatiyā|

karaṇakārakai tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

agginā kuṭiṃ jhāpaiti, manasā cai paduṭṭhaina, manasā cai pasan‍naina, kāyaina kammaṃ karauti|

287, 299. sahādiyaugai  ca|

sahādiyaugatthai ca tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

sahāpi gaggaina saṅghau upausathaṃ karaiyya, vināpi gaggaina, mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ, sahassaina samaṃ mitā|

288, 293. kattari ca|

kattari ca tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

rañ‍ñā hatau pausau, yakkhaina din‍nau varau, ahinā daṭṭhau narau|

289, 297. haitvatthai ca|

haitvatthai ca tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

an‍naina vasati, dhammaina vasati, vij‍jāya vasati, sak‍kāraina vasati|

290, 298. sattamyatthai ca|

sattamyatthai ca tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

taina  kālaina, taina samayaina| (yaina kālaina, yaina samayaina,) taina khau pana samayaina|

291, 299. yainaṅgavikārau|

yaina byādhimatā aṅgaina aṅginau vikārau lakkhīyatai| tattha tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

akkhinā kāṇau, hatthaina kuṇī, kāṇaṃ passati naittaina, pādaina khañ‍jau, piṭṭhiyā khuj‍jau|

292, 300. visaisanai ca|

visaisanatthai ca tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

gauttaina gautamau nāthau, suvaṇṇaina abhirūpau, tapasā uttamau|

293, 301. sampadānai catutthī|

sampadānakārakai catutthīvibhatti hauti|

buddhassa vā dhammassa vā saṅghassa vā dānaṃ daiti, dātā hauti samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā|

294, 305. namauyaugādīsvapi ca|

namauyaugādīsvapi ca catutthīvibhatti hauti|

namau  tai buddha vīratthu, sautthi pajānaṃ, namau karauhi nāgassa, svāgataṃ tai mahārāja|

295, 307. apādānai pañ‍camī|

apādānakārakai pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti|

pāpā cittaṃ nivārayai, abbhā muttauva candimā, bhayā muc‍cati sau narau|

296, 314. kāraṇatthai ḍha|

kāraṇatthai ca pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti|

ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ adassanā|

297, 284. kammatthai dutiyā|

kammatthai dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

gāvaṃ hanati, vīhayau lunāti, satthaṃ karauti, ghaṭaṃ karauti, rathaṃ karauti, dhammaṃ suṇāti, buddhaṃ pūjaiti, vācaṃ bhāsatī, taṇḍulaṃ vacati, cauraṃ ghātaiti|

298, 287. kāladdhānamac‍cantasaṃyaugai|

kāladdhānaṃ ac‍cantasaṃyaugai dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

māsaṃ  maṃsaudanaṃ bhuñ‍jati, saradaṃ ramaṇīyā nadī, māsaṃ sajjhāyati, yaujanaṃ vanarāji, yaujanaṃ dīghau pabbatau, kausaṃ sajjhāyati|

ac‍cantasaṃyaugaiti kimatthaṃ? saṃvaccharai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jati|

299, 288. kammappavadhanīyayuttai|

kammappavacanīyayuttai dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau, pabbajitamanupabbajiṃsu|

300, 286. gati buddhi bhuja paṭha hara kara sayādīnaṃ kāritai vā|

gati buddhi bhuja paṭha hara kara sayādīnaṃ payaugai kāritai dutiyāvibhatti hauti vā|

purisau purisaṃ (gāmaṃ) gāmayati, purisau purisaina vā, purisau purisassa vā| aivaṃ baudhayati, bhaujayati, pāṭhayati, hārayati, kārayati, sayāpayati| aivaṃ sabbattha kāritai|

301, 215. sāmismiṃ chaṭṭhī|

sāmismiṃ chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti|

tassa  bhikkhunau paṭivīsau, tassa bhikkhunau pattau, tassa bhikkhunau cīvaraṃ, attanau mukhaṃ|

302, 319. aukāsai sattamī|

aukāsakārakai sattamīvibhatti hauti|

gambhīrai audakantikai, pāpasmiṃ ramati manau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati kulaputtau|

303, 321. sāmissarādhipati dāyāda sakkhīpatibhū pasutakusalaihi ca|

sāmī issara adhipati dāyāda sakkhīpatibhū pasutakusala ic‍caitaihi payaugai chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca|

gauṇānaṃ sāmī, gauṇaisu sāmī, gauṇānaṃ issarau, gauṇaisu issarau| gauṇānaṃ adhipati, gauṇaisu adhipati| gauṇānaṃ dāyādau, gauṇaisu dāyādau| gauṇānaṃ sakkhī, gauṇaisu sakkhī, gauṇānaṃ patibhū, gauṇaisu patibhū| gauṇānaṃ pasutau, gauṇaisu pasutau| gauṇānaṃ kusalau, gauṇaisu kusalau|

304, 322. niddhāraṇai ca|

niddhāraṇatthai ca chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca|

kaṇhā  gāvīnaṃ sampan‍nakhīratamā, kaṇhā gāvīsu sampan‍nakhīratamā| sāmā nārīnaṃ dassanīyatamā, sāmā nārīsu dassanīyatamā| manussānaṃ khattiyau sūratamau, manussaisu khattiyau sūratamau| pathikānaṃ dhāvantau sīghatamau, pathikaisu dhāvantau sīghatamau|

305, 323. anādarai ca|

anādarai chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca|

rudatau dārakassa pabbaji, rudantasmiṃ dārakai pabbaji|

306, 289. k‍vaci dutiyā chaṭṭhīnamatthai|

chaṭṭhīnamatthai k‍vaci dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

apissu maṃ aggivaissana tissau upamā paṭibhaṃsu|

307, 290. tatiyāsattamīnañ‍ca|

tatiyāsattamīnaṃ atthai ca k‍vaci dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

sacai maṃ samaṇau gautamau ālapissati, tvañ‍ca maṃ nābhibhāsasi| aivaṃ tatiyatthai|

pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā, aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā| aivaṃ sattamyatthai|

308, 317. chaṭṭhī  ca|

tatiyāsattamīnaṃ atthai ca k‍vaci chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti|

katau mai kalyāṇau, kataṃ mai pāpaṃ| aivaṃ tatiyatthai|

kusalā nac‍cagītassa sikkhitā cāturitthiyau, kusalau tvaṃ rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ| aivaṃ sattamyatthai|

k‍vacīti kimatthaṃ? yau vau ānanda mayā dhammau ca vinayau ca daisitau pañ‍ñattau, ānandau atthaisu vicakkhaṇau|

309, 318. dutiyāpañ‍camīnañ‍ca|

dutiyāpañ‍camīnañ‍ca atthai k‍vaci chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti|

tassa bhavanti vattārau, sahasā kammassa kattārau, aivaṃ dutiyatthai|

assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, kin‍nu khau ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, sabbai tasanti daṇḍassa, sabbai bhāyanti mac‍cunau, bhītau catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ ghauravisānaṃ, bhāyāmi ghauravisassa nāgassa| aivaṃ pañ‍camyatthai|

310, 324. kamma  karaṇa nimittatthaisu sattamī|

kammakaraṇanimittatthaisu sattamīvibhatti hauti|

sundarāvusau imai ājīvakā bhikkhūsu abhivādainti| aivaṃ kammatthai|

hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti, pattaisu piṇḍāya caranti, pathaisu gacchanti| aivaṃ karaṇatthai|

dīpi cammaisu hañ‍ñatai, kuñ‍jarau dantaisu hañ‍ñatai, aivaṃ nimittatthai|

311, 325. sampadānai ca|

sampadānai ca sattamīvibhatti hauti|

saṅghai din‍naṃ mahapphala, saṅghai gautamī daihi, saṅghai tai din‍nai ahañ‍caiva pūjitau bhavissāmi|

312, 326. pañ‍camyatthai ca|

pañ‍camyatthai ca sattamīvibhatti hauti|

kadalīsu gajai rakkhanti|

313, 327. kālabhāvaisu ca|

kālabhāvaisu ca kattari payuj‍jamānai sattamīvibhatti hauti|

pubbaṇhasamayai  gatau, sāyanhasamayai āgatau| bhikkhūsu bhaujīyamānaisu gatau, bhuttaisu āgatau| gausuduyhamānaisu gatau, duddhāsu āgatau|

314, 328. upa’jhādhikissaravacanai|

upaadhiic‍caitaisaṃ payaugai adhikaissaravacanai sattamīvibhatti hauti|

upa khāriyaṃ dauṇau, upa nikkhai kahāpaṇaṃ| adhi brahmadattai pañ‍cālā, adhi nac‍caisu gautamī, adhi daivaisu buddhau|

315, 329. maṇḍitu’ssuk‍kaisu tatiyā|

maṇḍitaussuk‍kaic‍caitaisvatthaisu tatiyāvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca|

ñāṇaina pasīditau, ñāṇasmiṃ vā pasīditau, ñāṇaina ussuk‍kau, ñāṇasmiṃ vā ussuk‍kau tathāgatau vā tathāgatagauttau vā|

iti nāmakappai kārakakappau chaṭṭhau kaṇḍau|

kārakakappau niṭṭhitau|

4. samāsakappa

sattamakaṇḍa

316, 331. nāmānaṃ  samāsau yuttatthau|

taisaṃ nāmānaṃ payuj‍jamānapadatthānaṃ yau yuttatthau, sau samāsasañ‍ñau hauti|

kathinadussaṃ, āgantukabhattaṃ, jīvitindriyaṃ, samaṇabrāhmaṇā, sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, brāhmaṇa gahapatikā|

nāmānamiti kimatthaṃ? daivadattau pacati, yañ‍ñadattau pacati|

yuttatthauti kimatthaṃ? bhatau rañ‍ñau puttau daivadattassa|

samāsaic‍canaina k‍vatthau? k‍vaci samāsantagatānamakārantau|

317, 332. taisaṃ vibhattiyau laupā ca|

taisaṃ yuttatthānaṃ samāsānaṃ vibhattiyau laupā ca haunti|

kathinadussaṃ, āgantukabhattaṃ|

taisaṃgahaṇaina samāsataddhitākhyātakitakānaṃ vibhattipac‍cayapadakkharāgamā ca laupā haunti| vāsiṭṭhau, vainataiyyau|

caggahaṇa mavadhāraṇatthaṃ, pabhaṅkarau, amataddadau, maidhaṅkarau, dīpaṅkarau|

318, 333. pakati cassa sarantassa|

luttāsu vibhattīsu assa sarantassa liṅgassa pakatirūpāni haunti|

cakkhusautaṃ, mukhanāsikaṃ, rājaputtau, rājapurisau|

319, 330. upasagganipātapubbakau abyayībhāvau|

upasagganipātapubbakau samāsau abyayībhāvasañ‍ñau hauti|

nagarassa  samīpai pavattati kathā iti upanagaraṃ, darathānaṃ abhāvau niddarathaṃ, makasānaṃ abhāvau nimmakasaṃ, vuḍḍhānaṃ paṭipāṭi yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, yai yai vuḍḍhā vā yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, jīvassa yattakau paricchaidau yāvajīvaṃ, cittamadhikic‍ca pavattanti tai dhammāti adhicittaṃ, pabbatassa tirau tiraupabbataṃ, sautassa pati pavattati nāvā iti patisautaṃ, pāsādassa antau antaupāsādaṃ|

abyayībhāvamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? aṃvibhattīna makārantā abyayībhāvā|

320, 335. sau napuṃsakaliṅgau|

sau abyayībhāvasamāsau napuṃsakaliṅgauva daṭṭhabbau|

kumārīsu adhikic‍ca pavattati kathā iti adhikumāri, vadhuyā samīpai pavattati kathā iti upavadhu, gaṅgāya samīpai pavattati kathā iti upagaṅgaṃ, maṇikāya samīpai pavattati kathā iti upamaṇikaṃ|

321, 349. digussaikattaṃ|

digussa samāsassa aikattaṃ hauti, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca|

tayau  laukā tilaukaṃ, tayau daṇḍā tidaṇḍaṃ, tīṇi nayanāni tinayanaṃ, tayau siṅgā tisiṅgaṃ| catassau disā catuddisaṃ, pañ‍ca indriyāni pañ‍cindriyaṃ|

322, 359. tathā dvandai pāṇi tūriya yauggasainaṅgakhuddajantuka vividhaviruddha visabhāgatthādīnañ‍ca|

tathā dvandai samāsai pāṇi tūriya yaugga sainaṅgakhuddajantuka vividhaviruddha visabhāgatthaic‍caivamādīnaṃ aikattaṃ hauti, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca|

taṃ yathā? cakkhu ca sautañ‍ca cakkhusautaṃ, mukhañ‍ca nāsikā ca mukhanāsikaṃ, chavi ca maṃsañ‍ca lauhitañ‍ca chavimaṃsalauhitaṃ| aivaṃ pāṇyaṅgatthai|

saṅkhau ca paṇavau ca saṅkhapaṇavaṃ, gītañ‍ca vāditañ‍ca gītavāditaṃ, daddari ca ḍiṇḍimau ca daddariḍiṇḍimaṃ| aivaṃ tūriyaṅgatthai|

phālau ca pācanañ‍ca phālapācanaṃ, yugañ‍ca naṅgalañ‍ca yuganaṅgalaṃ| aivaṃ yauggaṅgatthai|

asi ca cammañ‍ca asicammaṃ, dhanu ca kalāpau ca dhanukalāpaṃ, hatthī ca assau ca hatthiassaṃ, rathau ca pattikau ca rathapattikaṃ| aivaṃ sainaṅgatthai|

ḍaṃsā  ca makasā ca ḍaṃsamakasaṃ, kunthau ca kipil‍likau ca kunthakipil‍likaṃ, kīṭau ca sarīsapau ca kīṭasarīsapaṃ| aivaṃ khuddajantukatthai|

ahi ca nakulau ca ahinakulaṃ, biḷārau ca mūsikau ca biḷāramūsikaṃ, kākau ca ulūkau ca kākaulūkaṃ| aivaṃ vividhaviruddhatthai|

sīlañ‍ca pañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sīlapañ‍ñāṇaṃ, samathau ca vipassanā ca samathavipassanaṃ, vij‍jā ca caraṇañ‍ca vij‍jācaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ visabhāgatthai|

ādiggahaṇaṃ kimatthaṃ? dāsī ca dāsau ca dāsidāsaṃ, itthī ca pumā ca itthipumaṃ, pattau ca cīvarañ‍ca pattacīvaraṃ, chattañ‍ca upāhanā ca chattupāhanaṃ, tikañ‍ca catuk‍kañ‍ca tikacatuk‍kaṃ, vainau ca rathakārau ca vainarathakāraṃ, sākuṇikau ca māgavikau ca savakuṇikamāgavikaṃ, dīghau ca majjhimau ca dīghamajjhimaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

323, 360. vibhāsā rukkha tiṇa pasukha na dhañ‍ña janapadādīnañ‍ca|

rukkhatiṇa pasu dhana dhañ‍ña janapadaic‍caivamādīnaṃ vibhāsā aikattaṃ hauti, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca dvandai samāsai| assatthau  ca kapītanau ca assatthakapītanaṃ, assatthakapītanā vā| usīrañ‍ca bīraṇañ‍ca usīrabīraṇaṃ, usīrabīraṇā vā| ajau ca aiḷakau ca ajaiḷakaṃ, ajaiḷakā vā| hirañ‍ñañ‍ca suvaṇṇañ‍ca hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ, hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇā vā| sāli ca yavau ca sāliyavaṃ, sāliyavā vā| kāsī ca kausalā ca kāsikausalaṃ, kāsikausalā vā|

ādiggahaṇaṃ kimatthaṃ? sāvaj‍jañ‍ca anavaj‍jañ‍ca sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jaṃ, sāvaj‍jā navaj‍jā vā| hīnañ‍ca paṇītañ‍ca hīnapaṇītaṃ| hīnapaṇītā vā| kaṇhau ca suk‍kau ca kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ, kaṇhasuk‍kā vā|

324, 339. dvipadai tulyādhikaraṇai kammadhārayau|

dvai padāni tulyādhikaraṇāni yadā samasyantai, tadā sau samāsau kammadhārayasañ‍ñau hauti|

mahantau  ca sau purisau cāti mahāpurisau, kaṇhau ca sau sappau cāti kaṇhasappau, nīlañ‍ca taṃ uppalañ‍cāti nīluppalaṃ, lauhitañ‍ca taṃ candanañ‍cāti lauhitacandanaṃ, brāhmaṇī ca sā dārikā cāti brāhmaṇadārikā, khattiyā ca sā kañ‍ñā cāti khattiyakañ‍ñā|

kammadhārayaic‍canaina k‍vatthau? kammadhārayasañ‍ñai ca|

325, 348. saṅkhyāpubbau digu|

saṅkhyāpubbau kammadhārayasamāsau digusañ‍ñau hauti|

tīṇi malāni timalaṃ, tīṇi phalāni tiphalaṃ, tayau laukā tilaukaṃ, tayau daṇḍā tidaṇḍaṃ, catassau disā catuddisaṃ, pañ‍ca indriyāni pañ‍cindriyaṃ, satta gaudāvariyau sattagaudāvaraṃ|

diguic‍canaina k‍vatthau? digussai kattaṃ|

326, 341. ubhai tappurisā|

ubhai digukammadhārayasamāsā tappurisasañ‍ñā haunti|

na brāhmaṇau abrāhmaṇau, na vasalau avasalau, na bhikkhu abhikkhu, na pañ‍cavassaṃ apañ‍cavassaṃ, na  pañ‍capūlī apañ‍capūlī, na sattagaudāvaraṃasattagaudāvaraṃ, na dasagavaṃ adasagavaṃ, na pañ‍cagavaṃ apañ‍cagavaṃ|

tappurisaic‍canaina k‍vatthau? attaṃ nassa tappurisai|

327, 351. amādayau parapadaibhi|

tā amādayau nāmaihi parapadaibhi yadā samasyantai, tadā sau samāsau tappurisasañ‍ñau hauti|

bhūmiṃ gatau bhūmigatau, sabbarattiṃ saubhaṇau sabbarattisaubhaṇau| apāyaṃ gatau apāyagatau, issaraina kataṃ issarakataṃ, sal‍laina viddhau sal‍laviddhau, kathinassa dussaṃ kathinadussaṃ, āgantukassa bhattaṃ āgantukabhattaṃ, maithunā apaitau maithunāpaitau, caurā bhayaṃ caurabhayaṃ, rañ‍ñau puttau rājaputtau, dhañ‍ñānaṃ rāsi dhañ‍ñarāsi, rūpai sañ‍ñā rūpasañ‍ñā, saṃsārai dukkhaṃ saṃsāradukkhaṃ|

328, 352. añ‍ñapadatthaisu bahubbīhi|

añ‍ñaisaṃ padānaṃ atthaisu dvai nāmāni bahūni nāmāni yadā samasyantai, tadā sau samāsau bahubbīhi sañ‍ñau hauti|

āgatā  samaṇā imaṃ saṅghārāmaṃ sauyaṃ āgatasamaṇau, saṅghārāmau| jitāni indriyāni anaina samaṇaina sauyaṃ jitindriyau, samaṇau| din‍nau suṅkau yassa rañ‍ñau sauyaṃ din‍nasuṅkau, rājā| niggatā janā asmā gāmā sauyaṃ niggatajanau, gāmau| chin‍nau hattau yassa purisassa sauyaṃ chin‍nahatthau, purisau| sampan‍nāni sassāni yasmiṃ janapadai sauyaṃ sampan‍nasassau, janapadau|

nigraudhassa parimaṇḍalau nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau, nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau iva parimaṇḍalau yau rājakumārau sauyaṃ nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau| atha vā nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau iva parimaṇḍalau yassa rājakumārassa sauyaṃ nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau, rājakumārau|

cakkhunau bhūtau cakkhubhūtau, cakkhubhūtau iva bhūtau yau bhagavā sauyaṃ cakkhubhūtau, bhagavā|

suvaṇṇassa vaṇṇau suvaṇṇavaṇṇau, suvaṇṇavaṇṇau viya vaṇṇau yassa bhagavatau sauyaṃ suvaṇṇavaṇṇau, bhagavā|

brahmunau sarau brahmassarau, brahmassarau viya sarau yassa bhagavatau sauyaṃ brahmassarau, bhagavā|

sayaṃ  patita paṇṇa pupphaphalavāyutauyāhārāti paṇṇañ‍ca pupphañ‍ca phalañ‍ca paṇṇapupphaphalāni, sayamaiva patitāni sayaṃpatitāni, sayaṃpatitāni ca tāni paṇṇapupphaphalāni caiti sayaṃpatitapaṇṇapupphaphalāni vāyu ca tauyañ‍ca vāyutauyāni, sayaṃpatitapaṇṇapupphaphalāni ca vāyutauyāni ca sayaṃpatitapaṇṇapupphaphalavāyutauyāni| sayaṃpatitapaṇṇapupphaphalavāyutauyāni āhārā yaisaṃ tai sayaṃpatitapaṇṇapupphaphalavāyutauyāhārā, isayau| yamaittha vattabbaṃ, taṃ haiṭṭhā vuttamaiva| ayaṃ pana dvandakammadhārayagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇapahubbīhi|

atha vā – sayaṃpatitapaṇṇapupphaphalavāyutauyaihi āhārā yaisaṃ tai sayaṃpatitapaṇṇapupphaphalavāyutauyāhārā| ayaṃ pana bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

nānā dumapatita pupphavāsita sānūti nānāpakārā dumā nānādumā, nānādumaihi patitāni nānādumapati tāni, nānādumapatitāni ca tāni pupphāni caiti nānādumapatitapupphāni, nānādumapatitapupphaihi vāsitā nānādumapatitapupphavāsitā, nānādumapatitapupphavāsitā sānū yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ nānādumapatitapupphavāsitasānu, pabbatarājā| ayaṃ pana kammadhārayatappurisagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

atha  vā – vāsitā sānū vāsitasānu, sāpaikkhattai satipi gamakattā samāsau| nānādumapatitapupphaihi vāsitasānū yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ nānādumapatitapupphavāsitasānu, pabbatarājā| ayaṃ pana bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

byālambambudharabinducumbitakūṭauti ambuṃ dhāraitīti ambudharau, kau sau? paj‍jun‍nau| vividhā ālambau byālambau, byālambau ca sau ambudharau cāti byālambambudharau, byālambambudharassa bindū byālambambudharabindū, byālambambudharabindūhi cumbitau byālambambudharabinducumbitau, byālambamburaibinducumbitau kūṭau yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ byālambambudharabinducumbitakūṭau| ayaṃ pana kammadhārayatappurisagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

atha vā – cumbitau kūṭau cumbitakūṭau, sāpaikkhattai satipi gamakattā samāsau| byālambambudharabindūhi cumbitakūṭau yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ byālambambudharabinducumbitakūṭau| ayaṃ pana bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

amita bala parak‍kamajutīti na mitā amitā, balañ‍ca parak‍kamau ca juti ca balaparak‍kamajutiyau, amitā balaparak‍kamajutiyau yassa sauyaṃ amitabalaparak‍kamajuti, ayaṃ pana kammadhārayadvandagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

pīṇaurakkhaṃsa  bāhūti urau ca akkhañ‍ca aṃsau ca bāhu ca urakkhaṃsabāhavau, pīṇā urakkhaṃsabāhavau yassa bhagavatau sauyaṃ pīṇaurakkhaṃsabāhu| ayaṃ pana dvandavabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

pīṇa gaṇḍa vadana thanūrujaghanāti gaṇḍau ca vadanañ‍ca thanau ca ūru ca jaghanañ‍ca gaṇḍavadanathanūrujaghanā, pīṇā gaṇḍavadanathanūrujaghanā yassā sāyaṃ pīṇagaṇḍavadanathanūrujaghanā| ayampi dvandagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

vavara surāsura garuḍa manuja bhujaga gandhabba makuṭa kūṭa cumbita sailasaṅghaṭṭita caraṇauti surā ca asurā ca garuḍā ca manujā ca bhujagā ca gandhabbā ca surāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbā, pavarā ca tai surāsuragaruḍamanujabhujaga gandhabbā caiti pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbā, pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbānaṃ makuṭāni pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭāni, pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭānaṃ kūṭāni pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭāni, pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭaisu cumbitā pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitā, pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitā ca tai sailā cāti pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitasailā, pavarasurāsura garuḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitasailaihi saṅghaṭṭitā pavarasurāsura garuḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitasailasaṅghaṭṭitā, pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitasailasaṅghaṭṭitā caraṇā yassa tathāgatassa sauyaṃ  pavarasurāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitasailasaṅghaṭṭitacaraṇau, tathāgatau, ayaṃ pana dvandakammadhārayatappurisagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

atha vā–saṅghaṭṭitā caraṇā saṅghaṭṭitacaraṇā, sāpaikkhattai satipi gamakattā samāsau| pavarasurāsura garuḍamanujabhujaga gandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitasailaihi saṅghaṭṭitacaraṇā yassa tathāgatassa sauyaṃ pavarasurāsuragaruḍa manujabhujaga gandhabbamakuṭakūṭacumbitasailasaṅghaṭṭita caraṇau| ayaṃ pana bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

catuddisauti catassau disā yassa sauyaṃ catuddisau, bhagavā|

pañ‍cacakkhūti pañ‍ca cakkhūni yassa tathāgatassa sauyaṃ pañ‍cacakkhu, tathāgatau|

dasabalauti dasa balāni yassa sauyaṃ dasabalau, bhagavā|

anantañāṇauti nassa antau anantaṃ, anantaṃ ñāṇaṃ yassa tathāgatassa sauyaṃ anantañāṇau, tathāgatau|

amita ghana sarīrauti na mitaṃ amitaṃ, ghanaṃ aiva sarīraṃ ghanasarīraṃ, amitaṃ ghanasarīraṃ yassa tathāgatassa sauyaṃ amitaghanasarīrau, tathāgatau|

amita bala parak‍kama pattauti na mitā amitā, balañ‍ca parak‍kamau ca balaparak‍kamā, amitā aiva balaparak‍kamā amitabalaparak‍kamā, amitabalaparak‍kamā pattā yaina sauyaṃ amitabalaparak‍kamapattau, bhagavā| ayaṃ pana kammadhārayadvandagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

matta bhamara gaṇa cumbita vikasitapupphaval‍lināgarukkhau pasaubhita kandarauti mattā aiva bhamarā mattabhamarā, mattabhamarānaṃ gaṇā mattabhamaragaṇā, mattabhamaragaṇaihi cumbitāni mattabhamaraṇacumbitāni, vikasitāni aiva pupphāni vikasitapupphāni, mattabhamaragaṇacumbitāni vikasitapupphāni yaisaṃ taihi mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphā, val‍li ca nāgarukkhau ca mal‍lināgarukkhā, mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphā ca tai val‍lināgarukkhā caiti mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphaval‍lināgarukkhā, mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphaval‍lināgarukkhaihi upasaubhitāni mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphaval‍lināgarukkhau pasaubhitāni, mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphaval‍lināgarukkhau pasaubhitāni kandarāni yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapuppha val‍lināgarukkhau pasau bhitakandarau, pabbatarājā| ayaṃ pana dvandakammadhārayatappurisagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

atha vā–upasaubhitāni kandarāni upasaubhitakandarāni, sāpaikkhattai satipi gamakattā samāsau| mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphaval‍lināgarukkhaihi upasaubhitakandarāni yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ mattabhamaragaṇacumbitavikasitapupphaval‍lināgarukkhau pasaubhitakandarau, pabbatarājā| ayaṃ pana bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

nānā  rukkha tiṇa patita pupphaupasaubhita kandarauti rukkhau ca tiṇañ‍ca rukkhatiṇāni, nānā pakārāni aiva rukkhatiṇāni nānārukkhatiṇāni, nānārukkhatiṇaihi patitāni nānārukkhatiṇapatitāni, nānārukkhatiṇapatitāni ca tāni pupphāni caiti nānārukkhatiṇapatitapupphāni, nānārukkhatiṇapatitapupphaihi upasaubhitāni nānārukkhatiṇapatita pupphaupasaubhitāni, nānārukkhatiṇapatitapupphaupasaubhitāni kandarāni yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ nānārukkhatiṇapatitapupphaupasaubhitakandarau, pabbatarājā| ayaṃ pana dvandakammadhārayatappurisagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

atha vā–upasaubhitāni aiva kandarāni upasaubhitakandarāni, (sāpaikkhattai satipi gamakattā samāsau|) nānārukkhatiṇapatitapupphaihi upasaubhitakandarāni yassa pabbatarājassa sauyaṃ nānārukkhatiṇapatitapupphaupasaubhitakandarau, pabbatarājā| ayaṃ pana bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

nānā musala phāla pabbata taru kaliṅgara sara dhanugadāsi taumarahatthāti musalau ca phālau ca pabbatau ca taru ca kaliṅgarau ca sarau ca dhanu ca gadā caasi ca taumarau, ca musalaphālapabbatatarukaliṅgarasaradhanugadāsitaumarā, nānā pakārā aiva musalaphālapabbatatarukaliṅgarasaradhanugadāsitaumarā nānāmusalaphālapabbatatarukaliṅgarasaradhanugadāsitaumarā, nānāmusalaphālapabbatatarukaliṅgarasaradhanugadāsitaumarā hatthaisu  yaisaṃ tai nānāmusalaphālapabbatatarukaliṅgara saradhanugadāsitaumarahatthā| ayaṃ pana dvandakammadhārayagabbhau bhin‍nādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

bahubbīhi ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? bahubbīhimhi ca|

329, 357. nāmānaṃ samuc‍cayau dvandau|

nāmānaṃ aikavibhattikānaṃ yau samuc‍cayau, sau dvandasañ‍ñau hauti|

candimā ca sūriyau ca candimasūriyā, samaṇau ca brāhmaṇau ca samaṇabrāhmaṇā, sāriputtau ca mauggal‍lānau ca sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, brāhmaṇau ca gahapatikau ca brāhmaṇagahapatikā, yamau ca varuṇau ca yamavaruṇā, kuvairau ca vāsavau ca kuvairavāsavā|

dvandaic‍canaina k‍vatthau? dvandaṭṭhā vā|

330, 340. mahataṃ mahā tulyādhikaraṇai padai|

taisaṃ mahantasaddānaṃ mahāādaisau hauti tulyādhikaraṇai padai|

mahantau  ca sau purisau cāti mahāpurisau, mahantī ca sā daivī cāti mahādaivī, mahantañ‍ca taṃ balañ‍cāti mahābalaṃ, mahantau ca sau nāgau cāti mahānāgau, mahantau ca sau yasau cāti mahāyasau, mahantañ‍ca taṃ padumavanañ‍cāti mahāpadumavanaṃ, mahantī ca sā nadī cāti mahānadī, mahantau ca sau maṇi cāti mahāmaṇi, mahantau ca sau gahapatikau cāti mahāgahapatikau, mahantañ‍ca taṃ dhanañ‍cāti mahādhanaṃ, mahantau ca sau puñ‍ñau cāti mahāpuñ‍ñau|

bahuvacanaggahaṇaina k‍vaci mahanta saddassa mahādaisau hauti| mahantañ‍ca taṃ phalañ‍cāti mahapphalaṃ, mahabbalaṃ, aivaṃ mahaddhanaṃ, mahabbhayaṃ|

331, 353. itthiyaṃ bhāsitapumitthī pumāva cai|

itthiyaṃ tulyādhikaraṇai padai cai bhāsitapumitthī pumāva daṭṭhabbā|

dīghā jaṅghā yassa sauyaṃ dīghajaṅghau, kalyāṇabhariyau, pahūtapañ‍ñau|

bhāsitapupaiti  kimatthaṃ? brāhmaṇabandhu ca sā bhariyā cātibrāhmaṇabandhubhariyā|

332, 343. kammadhārayasañ‍ñai ca|

kammadhārayasañ‍ñai ca samāsai itthiyaṃ tulyādhikaraṇai padai pubbai bhāsitapumitthī cai, pumāva daṭṭhabbā| brāhmaṇadārikā, khattiyakañ‍ñā, khattiyakumārikā|

bhāsitapumaiti kimatthaṃ? khattiyabandhudārikā, brāhmaṇabandhudārikā|

333, 344. attaṃ nassa tappurisai|

nassa padassa tappurisai uttarapadai attaṃ hauti|

nabrāhmaṇau abrāhmaṇau, avasalau, abhikkhu, apañ‍cavassaṃ, apañ‍cagavaṃ|

334, 345. sarai ana|

nassa padassa tappurisai ana ādaisau hauti sarai parai|

na assau anassau, anissarau, anariyau|

335, 346. kada  kussa|

kuic‍caitassa kada hauti sarai parai|

kucchitaṃ an‍naṃ kadan‍naṃ, kucchitaṃ asanaṃ kadassanaṃ|

saraiti kimatthaṃ? kucchitā dārā yaisaṃ (apuñ‍ñakārānaṃ) tai hauntīti kudārā, kujanā| aivaṃ kuputtā, kugaihā, kuvatthā, kudāsā|

336, 347. kā’ppatthaisu ca|

kuic‍caitassa kā hauti appatthaisu ca|

kālavaṇaṃ, kāpupphaṃ|

bahuvacanaggahaṇaṃ kimatthaṃ? kuic‍caitassa anappatatthaisupi k‍vaci kā hauti, kāpurisā|

337, 350. k‍vaci samāsantagatānamakārantau|

samāsantagatānaṃ nāmānamantau sarau k‍vaci akārau hauti|

daivānaṃ rājā daivarājau, daivarājā, daivānaṃ sakhā daivasakhau, daivasakhā, pañ‍ca ahāni pañ‍cāhaṃ, sattāhaṃ, pañ‍cagavaṃ, chattupāhanaṃ, upasaradaṃ, visālakkhau, vimukhau|

kāraggahaṇaṃ  kimatthaṃ? ākāranta ikārantā ca haunti, pac‍cakkhā dhammā yassa sauyanti pac‍cakkhadhammā, surabhinau gandhau surabhigandhi, sundarau gandhau sugandhi, pūtinau gandhau pūtigandhi, kucchitau gandhau kugandhi, duṭṭhu gandhau yassa sauyanti dugandhi, pūti aiva gandhau pūtigandhi|

nadīantā ca kattuantā ca kapac‍cayau hauti samāsantai|

bahū nadiyau yasmiṃ sauyaṃ bahunadikau, janapadau| bahavau kattārau yassa sauyaṃ bahukattukau, purisau|

338, 356. nadimhā ca|

nadimhā ca kapac‍cayau hauti samāsantai|

bahū nadiyau yasmiṃ sauyanti bahunadikau| bahū kantiyau yassa sauyanti bahukantikau| bahunārikau|

339, 358. jāyāya tudaṃ jāni patimhi|

jāyāic‍caitāya tudaṃ jāniic‍caitai ādaisā haunti patimhi parai|

tudaṃpatī, jānipatī|

340, 355. dhanumhā ca|

dhanumhā ca āpac‍cayau hauti samāsantai|

gāṇḍīvau  dhanu yassa sauyaṃ gāṇḍīvadhanvā|

341, 336. aṃ vibhattīnamakārantā abyayībhāvā|

tasmā akārantā abyayībhāvasamāsā parāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ k‍vaci aṃ hauti|

adhicittaṃ, yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, upakumbhaṃ, yāvajīvaṃ, tiraupabbataṃ, tiraupākāraṃ, tiraukuṭṭaṃ, antaupāsādaṃ|

k‍vacīti kimatthaṃ? adhicittassa bhikkhunau|

342, 337. sarau rassau napuṃsakai|

napuṃsakai vattamānassa abyayībhāvasamāsassa liṅgassa sarau rassau hauti|

kumārīsu adhikic‍ca pavattati kathā iti adhikumāri| upavadhu, upagaṅgaṃ, upamaṇikaṃ|

343, 338. añ‍ñasmā laupau ca|

añ‍ñasmā abyayībhāvasamāsā anakārantā parāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau ca hauti|

adhitthi, adhikumāri, upavadhu|

iti nāmakappai samāsakappau sattamau kaṇḍau|

samāsakappau niṭṭhitau|

5. taddhitakappa

aṭṭhamakaṇḍa

344, 361. vāṇa’pac‍cai|

ṇapac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’ mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

vasiṭṭhassa apac‍caṃ vāsiṭṭhau, vasiṭṭhassa apac‍caṃ vā, vasiṭṭhassa apac‍caṃ vāsiṭṭhī, vasiṭṭhassa apac‍caṃ vāsiṭṭhaṃ| aivaṃ bhāradvājau, bhāradvājī, bhāradvājaṃ| gautamau, gautamī, gautamaṃ| vāsudaivau, vāsudaivī, vāsudaivaṃ| bāladaivau, bāladaivī, bāladaivaṃ| vaisāmittau, vaisāmittī vaisāmittaṃ|

345, 366. ṇāyana ṇāna vacchāditau|

tasmā vacchāditau gauttagaṇatau ṇāyanaṇānapac‍cayā haunti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

vacchassa apac‍caṃvacchāyanau, vacchānau, vacchassa apac‍caṃ vā, vacchassa apac‍caṃ vacchāyanī, vacchānī, vacchassa apac‍caṃ vacchāyanaṃ, vacchānaṃ| sakaṭassa apac‍caṃ sākaṭāyanau, sākaṭānau, sakaṭassa apac‍caṃ vā, sākaṭāyanī, sā kaṭānī, sākaṭāyanaṃ, sākaṭānaṃ| aivaṃ kaṇhāyanau, kaṇhānau, kaṇhassa apac‍caṃ vā| kaṇhāyanī, kaṇhānī, kaṇhāyanaṃ, kaṇhānaṃ| aggivaissāyanau, aggivaissānau, aggivaissāyanī, aggivaissānī, aggivaissāyanaṃ, aggivaissānaṃ| gacchāyanau, gacchānau, gacchāyanī, gacchānī, gacchāyanaṃ, gacchānaṃ| kappāyanau, kappānau, kappāyanī, kappānī, kappāyanaṃ, kappānaṃ| mauggal‍lāyanau, mauggal‍lānau, mauggal‍lāyanī, mauggal‍lānī, mauggal‍lāyanaṃ, mauggal‍lānaṃ| muñ‍cāyanau, muñ‍cānau, muñ‍cāyanī, muñ‍cānī, muñ‍cāyanaṃ, muñ‍cānaṃ| saṅghāyanau, saṅghānau, saṅghāyanī, saṅghānī, saṅghāyanaṃ, saṅghānaṃ| laumāyanau, laumānau, laumāyanī, laumānī, laumāyanaṃ, laumānaṃ, sākamāyanau, sākamānau, sākamāyanī, sākamānī, sākamāyanaṃ, sākamānaṃ| nārāyanau, nārānau, nārāyanī, nārānī, nārāyanaṃ, nārānaṃ| caurāyanaucaurānau, caurāyanī, caurānī, caurāyanaṃ, caurānaṃ, āvasālāyanau, āvasālānau, āvasālāyanī, āvasālānī, āvasālāyanaṃ, āvasālānaṃ| dvaipāyanau, dvaipānau, dvaipāyanī, dvaipānī, dvaipāyanaṃ, dvaipānaṃ| kuñ‍cāyanau, kuñ‍cānau, kuñ‍cāyanī, kuñ‍cānī, kuñ‍cāyanaṃ, kuñ‍cānaṃ| kac‍cāyanau, kac‍cānau, kac‍cāyanī, kac‍cānī, kac‍cāyanaṃ, kac‍cānaṃ|

346, 367. ṇaiyyau  kattikādīhi|

taihi gauttagaṇaihi kattikādīhi ṇaiyyapac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

kattikāya apac‍caṃ kattikaiyyau, kattikāya apac‍caṃ vā| aivaṃ vainataiyyau, rauhiṇaiyyau, gaṅgaiyyau, kaddamaiyyau, nādaiyyau, ālaiyyau, āhaiyau, kāmaiyyau, suciyā apac‍caṃ saucaiyyau, sālaiyyau, bālaiyyau, mālaiyyau, kālaiyyau|

347, 368. atau ṇi vā|

tasmā akāratau ṇipac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

dakkhassa apac‍caṃ dakkhi, dakkhassa apac‍caṃ vā| duṇassa apac‍caṃ dauṇi, duṇassa apac‍caṃ vā, aivaṃ vāsavi, sakyaputti, nāṭaputti, dāsaputti, dāsavi, vāruṇi, gaṇḍi, bāladaivi, pāvaki, jainadatti, buddhi, dhammi, saṅghi, kappi, anuruddhi|

vāti vikappanatthaina ṇikapac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| sakyaputtassa apac‍caṃ sakyaputtikau| aivaṃ nāṭaputtikau, jainadattikau|

148, 371. ṇavau’  pak‍vādīhi|

upakuic‍caivamādīhi ṇavapac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

upakussa apac‍caṃ aupakavau, upakussa apac‍caṃ vā| manunau apac‍caṃ mānavau, manunau apac‍caṃ vā| bhaggussa apac‍caṃ bhaggavau, bhaggussa apac‍caṃ vā, paṇḍussa apac‍caṃ paṇḍavau, paṇḍussa apac‍caṃ vā, bahussa apac‍caṃ bāhavau, bahussa apac‍caṃ vā|

349, 372. ṇaira vidhavāditau|

tasmā vidhavāditau ṇairapac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

vidhavāya apac‍caṃ vaidhavairau, vidhavāya apac‍caṃ vā| bandhukiyā apac‍caṃ bandhukairau, bandhukiyā apac‍caṃ vā| samaṇassa apac‍caṃ sāmaṇairau, samaṇassa apac‍caṃ vā| aivaṃ sāmaṇairī, sāmaṇairaṃ, nāḷikairau, nāḷikairī, nāḷikairaṃ|

350, 373. yaina vā saṃsaṭṭhaṃ tarati carati vahati ṇikau|

yaina vā saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, yaina vā tarati, yaina vā carati, yaina vā vahati ic‍caitaisvatthaisu ṇikapac‍cayau hauti vā|

tilaina  saṃsaṭṭhaṃ bhaujanaṃ tailikaṃ, tilaina saṃsaṭṭhaṃ vā| aivaṃ gauḷikaṃ, ghātikaṃ|

nāvāya taratīti nāvikau, nāvāya tarati vā| aivaṃ auḷumpikau|

sakaṭaina caratīti sākaṭikau, sakaṭaina carati vā| aivaṃ pattikau, daṇḍikau, dhammikau, pādikau|

sīsaina vahatīti sīsikau, sīsaina vahati vā| aṃsaina vahatīti aṃsikau, aṃsaina vahati vā| aivaṃ khandhikau, aṅgulikau|

vāti vikappanatthaina añ‍ñaisupi ṇikapac‍cayau hauti| rājagahai vasatīti rājagahikau, rājagahai vasati vā| rājagahai jātau rājagahikau, rājagahai jātau vā| aivaṃ māgadhikau, sāvatthikau, kāpila vatthikau, pāṭaliputtikau, vaisālikau|

351, 374. tamadhītai tainakatādi san‍nidhāna niyauga sippa bhaṇḍa jīvikatthaisu ca|

tamadhītai, tainakatādiatthai, tamhi san‍nidhānā, tattha niyuttau, tamassa sippaṃ, tamassa bhaṇḍaṃ, tamassa jīvikaṃ ic‍caitaisvatthaisu ca ṇikapac‍cayau hauti vā|

vinayamadhītai  vainayikau, vinayamadhītai vā| aivaṃ suttantikau, ābhidhammikau, vaiyyākaraṇikau|

kāyaina kataṃ kammaṃ kāyikaṃ, kāyaina kataṃ kammaṃ vā| aivaṃ vācasikaṃ, mānasikaṃ|

sarīrai san‍nidhānā vaidanā sārīrikā, sarīrai san‍nidhānā vā, aivaṃ mānasikā|

dvārai niyuttau dauvārikau, dvārai niyuttau vā| aivaṃ bhaṇḍāgārikau, nāgarikau, nāvakammikau|

vīṇā assa sippaṃ vaiṇikau, vīṇā assa sippaṃ vā| aivaṃ pāṇavikau, maudiṅgikau, vaṃsikau|

gandhau assa bhaṇḍaṃ gandhikau, gandhau assa bhaṇḍaṃ vā| aivaṃ tailikau, gauḷikau|

urabbhaṃ hantvā jīvatīti aurabbhikau, urabbhaṃ hantvā jīvati vā| magaṃ hantvā jīvatīti māgavikau, magaṃ hantvā jīvati vā| aivaṃ saukarikau, sākuṇikau|

ādiggahaṇaina añ‍ñatthāpi ṇika pac‍cayau yaujaitabbau| jālaina hatau jālikau, jālaina hatau vā|

suttaina bandhau suttikau, suttaina bandhau vā|

cāpau  assa āvudhau cāpikau, cāpau assa āvudhau vā| aivaṃ taumarikau, muggarikau, mausalikau|

vātau assa ābādhau vātikau, vātau assa ābādhau vā| aivaṃ saimhikau, pittikau|

buddhai pasan‍nau buddhikau, buddhai pasan‍nau vā| aivaṃ dhammikau, saṅghikau|

buddhassa santakaṃ buddhikaṃ, buddhassa santakaṃ vā| aivaṃ dhammikaṃ, saṅghikaṃ|

vatthaina kītaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ vatthikaṃ, vatthaina kītaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ vā| aivaṃ kumbhikaṃ, phālikaṃ, kiṃ kaṇikaṃ, sauvaṇṇikaṃ|

kumbhau assa parimāṇaṃ kumbhikaṃ, kumbhau assa parimāṇaṃ vā|

kumbhassa rāsi kumbhikaṃ, kumbhassa rāsi vā|

kumbhaṃ arahatīti kumbhikau, kumbhaṃ arahati vā|

akkhaina dibbatīti akkhikau, akkhaina dibbati vā| aivaṃ sālākikau, tindukikau, ambaphalikau| kapiṭṭhaphalikau, nāḷikairikau ic‍caivamādi|

352, 376. ṇa rāgā tassai damañ‍ñatthaisu ca|

ṇapac‍cayau hauti vā rāgamhā ‘‘taina rattaṃ’’ ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai, ‘‘tassaidaṃ’’ añ‍ñatthaisu ca|

kasāvaina  rattaṃ vatthaṃ kāsāvaṃ, kasāvaina rattaṃ vatthaṃ vā| aivaṃ kausumbhaṃ, hāliddaṃ, pāṭaṅgaṃ, rattaṅga, mañ‍jiṭṭhaṃ, kuṅkumaṃ|

sūkarassa idaṃ maṃsaṃ saukaraṃ, sūkarassa idaṃ maṃsaṃ vā| aivaṃ māhisaṃ|

udumbarassa avidūrai pavattaṃ vimānaṃ audumbaraṃ, udumbarassa avidūrai pavattaṃ vimānaṃ vā|

vidisāya avidūrai nivāsau vaidisau, vidisāya avidūrai nivāsau vā|

mathurāya jātau māthurau, mathurāya jātau vā|

mathurāya āgatau māthurau, mathurāya āgatau vā|

kattikāya niyuttau māsau kattikau, kattikāya niyuttau māsau vā| aivaṃ māgasirau, phussau, māghau, phaggunau, cittau, vaisākhau, jaiṭṭhau, āsaḷhau, sāvaṇau, bhaddau, assayujau|

na vuddhi nīlapītādau, pac‍cayai saṇakārakai|

phakārau phussasaddassa, ‘‘sirau’’ti sirasaṃ vadai|

sikkhānaṃ  samūhau sikkhau, bhikkhānaṃ samūhau bhikkhau| aivaṃ kāpautau, māyūrau, kaukilau|

buddhai, assa daivatā buddhau| aivaṃ bhaddau, mārau, māhindau, vaissavaṇau, yāmau, saumau, nārāyaṇau|

saṃvaccharamadhītai saṃvaccharau| aivaṃ mauhuttau, naimittau, aṅgavij‍jau, vaiyyākaraṇau, chandau, bhāssau, candau|

vasādānaṃ visayau daisau vāsādau| aivaṃ kumbhau, sākuntau, ātisārau|

udumbarā asmiṃ padaisai santīti audumbarau| sāgaraihi nibbattau sāgarau| sāgalamassa nivāsau sāgalau| mathurā assa nivāsau māthurau| mathurāya issarau māthurau| ic‍caivamādayau yaujaitabbā|

353, 378. jātādīnamimiyā ca|

jātaic‍caivamādīnamatthai imaiyapac‍cayā haunti|

pacchā jātau pacchimau| aivaṃ antimau, majjhimau, purimau, uparimau, haiṭṭhimau, gaupphimau| baudhisattajātiyā jātau baudhisattajātiyau, aivaṃ  assajātiyau, hatthijātiyau, manussajātiyau|

ādiggahaṇaina niyuttatthāditaupi tadassatthāditaupi ima iya ika ic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti| antau niyuttau antimau| aivaṃ antiyau, antikau|

puttau assa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti puttimau| aivaṃ puttiyaiva, puttikau, kappimau, kappiyau, kappikau|

caggahaṇaina kiyapac‍cayau hauti niyuttatthai| jātiyaṃ niyuttau jātikiyau, andhai niyuttau andhakiyau, jātiyā andhau jac‍candhau, jac‍candhai niyuttau jac‍candhakiyau|

354, 379. samūhatthai kaṇa ṇā|

samūhatthai kaṇa ṇaic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti|

rājaputtānaṃ samūhau rājaputtakau| aivaṃ rājaputtau, mānussakau, mānussau, māyūrakau, māyūrau, māhiṃsakau, māhiṃsau|

355, 380. gāma jana bandhu sahāyādīhitā|

gāma jana bandhu sahāyaic‍caivamādīhi tāpac‍cayau hauti samūhatthai|

gāmānaṃ  samūhau gāmatā| aivaṃ janatā, bandhutā, sahāyatā, nagaratā|

356, 381. tadassa ṭhānamiyau ca|

‘‘tadassa ṭhāna’’ mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai iyapac‍cayau hauti|

madanassa ṭhānaṃ madaniyaṃ, bandhanassa ṭhānaṃ bandhaniyaṃ, mucchanassa ṭhānaṃ mucchaniyaṃ, aivaṃ rajaniyaṃ, kamaniyaṃ, gamaniyaṃ, dussaniyaṃ, dassaniyaṃ|

357, 382. upamatthāyitattaṃ|

upamatthai āyitattapac‍cayau hauti|

dhūmau viya dissati aduṃ vanaṃ tadidaṃ dhūmāyitattaṃ, timiraṃ viya dissati aduṃ vanaṃ tadidaṃ timirāyitattaṃ|

358, 383. tan‍nissitatthai lau|

‘‘tan‍nissitatthai, tadassa ṭhāna’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai ca lapac‍cayau hauti|

duṭṭhu  nissitaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ, vaidaṃ nissitaṃ vaidal‍laṃ, duṭṭhu ṭhānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ, vaidassa ṭhānaṃ vaidal‍laṃ|

359, 384. ālu tabbahulai|

ālupac‍cayau hauti tabbahulatthai|

abhijjhā assa pakati abhijjhālu, abhijjhā assa bahulā vā abhijjhālu| aivaṃ sītālu, dhajālu, dayālu|

360, 387. ṇya tta tā bhāvai tu|

ṇyattatāic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti bhāvatthai|

alasassa bhāvau ālasyaṃ, araugassa bhāvau āraugyaṃ| paṃsukūlikassa bhāvau paṃsukūlikattaṃ, anaudarikassa bhāvau anaudarikattaṃ| saṅgaṇikārāmassa bhāvau saṅgaṇikārāmatā, niddārāmassa bhāvau niddārāmatā|

tuggahaṇaina ttanapac‍cayau hauti| puthuj‍janattanaṃ, vaidanattanaṃ|

361, 388. ṇa  visamādīhi|

ṇapac‍cayau hauti visamādīhi ‘‘tassa bhāvau’’ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

362, 389. ramaṇīyāditau kaṇa|

363, 390. visaisai taratamisikiyiṭṭhā|

visaisatthai tara tama isika iya iṭṭhaic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti|

sabbai imai pāpā, ayamimaisaṃ visaisaina pāpauti pāpatarau| aivaṃ pāpatamau, pāpisikau, pāpiyau, pāpiṭṭhau|

364, 398. tadassatthīti vī ca|

365, 399. tapāditau sī|

tapāditau sīpac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassatthi’’ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

tapau yassa atthi tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti tapassī| aivaṃ yasassī, taijassī|

266, 400. daṇḍāditau ikaī|

367, 401. madhvāditau rau|

madhuic‍caivamāditau rapac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassatthi’’ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

madhu  yassa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti madhurau| aivaṃ kuñ‍jarau, muggarau, mukharau, susirau, (sīsarau, sukarau, suṅkarau), subharau, sucirau, rucirau|

368, 402. guṇāditau vantu|

369, 403. satyādīhi mantu|

satiic‍caivamādīhi mantupac‍cayauhauti ‘‘tadassatthi’’ ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

370, 405. saddhāditauṇa|

371, 404. āyussukārāsa mantumhi|

372, 385. tappakativacanai mayau|

suvaṇṇaina pakataṃ kammaṃ sauvaṇṇamayaṃ| aivaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ jatumayaṃ, rajatamayaṃ, iṭṭhakamayaṃ, ayaumayaṃ, mattikā mayaṃ, dārumayaṃ, gaumayaṃ|

373, 406. saṅkhyāpūraṇai mau|

374, 408. sa  chassavā|

chassa sakārādaisau hauti vā saṅkhyāpūraṇatthai|

chan‍naṃ pūraṇau saṭṭhau, chaṭṭhau vā|

375, 412. aikāditau dasassī|

aikāditau dasassa antai īpac‍cayau hauti vā saṅkhyāpūraṇatthai|

aikau ca dasa ca aikādasa, aikādasan‍naṃ pūraṇī aikādasī| pañ‍caca dasa ca pañ‍cadasa, pañ‍cadasan‍naṃ pūraṇī pañ‍cadasī| cattārau ca dasa ca catuddasa, catuddasan‍naṃ pūraṇī cātuddasī|

pūraṇaiti kimatthaṃ? aikādasa, pañ‍cadasa|

376, 257. dasai sau nic‍cañ‍ca|

dasasaddai parai nic‍caṃ chassa sau hauti|

sauḷasa|

389, 413. gaṇanai dasassa dviticatupañ‍cachasattaaṭṭhanavakānaṃ vī ti cattāra pañ‍ñā cha sattāsanavā yausu, yaunañ‍cīsamāsaṃ ṭhi ri tī tuti|

391, 423. yadanupapan‍nā nipātanā sijjhanti|

yai saddā aniddiṭṭhalakkhaṇā, akkharapadabyañ‍janatau, itthipumanapuṃsakaliṅgatau, nāmupasagganipātatau, abyayībhāvasamāsataddhitākhyātatau, gaṇanasaṅkhyākālakārakappayaugasañ‍ñātau, sandhipakativuddhilaupāgamavikāraviparitatau, vibhattivibhajanatau ca, tai nipātanā sijjhanti|

392, 418. dvāditau kau’naikatthaica|

393, 415. dasadasakaṃ sataṃ dasakānaṃ sataṃ sahassañ‍ca yaumhi|

394, 416. yāva taduttari dasaguṇitañ‍ca|

395, 417. sakanāmaihi|

402, 377. āttañ‍ca|

yūnamiti kimatthaṃ? apāyaisu jātau āpāyi kau|

403, 354. k‍vacādimajjhuttarānaṃ dīgharassā pac‍cayaisu ca|

k‍vaci ādimajjhauttaraic‍caitaisaṃ dīgharassā haunti pac‍cayaisu ca apac‍cayaisu ca|

ādidīghau tāva – pākārau, nīvārau, pāsādau, pākaṭau, pātimaukkhau, pāṭikaṅkhau ic‍caivamādi|

majjhaidīghau  tāva – aṅgamāgadhikau, aurabbhamāgavīkau ic‍caivamādi|

uttaradīghau tāva – khantī paramaṃ tapau titikkhā, añ‍janā giri, kauṭarā vanaṃ, aṅgulī ic‍caivamādi|

ādirassau tāva – pagaiva ic‍caivamādi|

majjhairassau tāva – sumaidhasau suvaṇṇadharaihi ic‍caivamādi|

uttararassau tāva – bhauvādi nāma sau hauti, yathābhāvi guṇaina sau ic‍caivamādi| añ‍ñaipi yathājinavacanānuparaudhaina yaujaitabbā|

caggahaṇaina apac‍cayaisu cāti atthaṃ samuc‍caiti,

404, 370. taisu vuddhilaupāgamavikāraviparītādaisā ca|

taisu ādimajjhuttaraisu yathājinavacanānuparaudhaina k‍vaci vuddhi hauti, k‍vaci laupau hauti, k‍vaci āgamau hauti, k‍vaci vikārau hauti, k‍vaci viparītau hauti, k‍vaci ādaisau hauti|

ādivuddhi tāva – ābhidhammikau, vainataiyyau ic‍caivamādi|

majjhaivuddhi  tāva – sukhasaiyyaṃ, sukhakāri dānaṃ, sukhakāri sīlaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

uttaravuddhi tāva – kāliṅgau, māgadhikau, pac‍cakkhadhammā ic‍caivamādi|

ādilaupau tāva – tālīsaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

majjhailaupau tāva – kattukāmau, kumbhakāraputtau, vaidal‍laṃ ic‍caivamādi|

uttaralaupau tāva – bhikkhu, bhikkhunī ic‍caivamādi|

ādiāgamau tāva – vuttau bhagavatā ic‍caivamādi|

majjhaiāgamau tāva – sasīlavā, sapañ‍ñavāic‍caivamādi|

uttaraāgamau tāva – vaidal‍laṃ ic‍caivamādi|

ādivikārau tāva – ārisyaṃ, āṇyaṃ, āsabhaṃ, aj‍javaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

majjhaivikārau tāva – varārisyaṃ, parārisyaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

uttaravikārau tāva – yāni, tāni, sukhāni ic‍caivamādi|

ādiviparītau  tāva – uggatai sūriyai uggacchati ic‍caivamādi|

majjhaiviparītau tāva – samuggacchati, samuggatai sūriyai ic‍caivamādi|

uttaraviparītau tāva – digu, diguṇaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

ādiādaisau tāva – yūnaṃ ic‍caivamādi|

majjhaiādaisau tāva – nyāyaugā ic‍caivamādi|

uttaraādaisau tāva – sabbasaiyyau, sabbasaiṭṭhau, cittaṃ ic‍caivamādi| aivaṃ yathājinavacanānuparaudhaina sabbattha yaujaitabbā|

405, 365. ayuvaṇṇānañ‍cāyau vuddhi|

a iti akārau, i īiti ivaṇṇau, u ūiti uvaṇṇau, taisaṃ akāraivaṇṇuvaṇṇānaṃ ā ai auvuddhiyau haunti yathāsaṅkhyaṃ, ā ī ūvuddhi ca|

ābhidhammikau, vainataiyyau, auḷumpikau|

puna vuddhiggahaṇaṃ kimatthaṃ? uttarapadavuddhibhāvatthaṃ, aṅgamagadhaihi āgatāti aṅgamāgadhikā| nigamajanapadaisu jātāti  naigamajānapadā| purimajanapadaisu jātāti paurimajānapadā| sattāhai niyuttauti sattāhikā, catuvij‍jai niyuttauti cātuvij‍jikā| ic‍caivamādī yaujaitabbā|

vuddhiic‍canaina k‍vatthau? vuddhādisarassa vā’saṃyaugantassa saṇai ca|

iti nāmakappai taddhitakappau aṭṭhamau kaṇḍau|

taddhitakappau niṭṭhitau|

6. ākhyātakappa

paṭhamakaṇḍa

(ka)

ākhyātasāgaramathaj‍jatanītaraṅgaṃ,

dhātuj‍jalaṃ vikaraṇāgamakālamīnaṃ|

laupānubandhariyamatthavibhāgatīraṃ,

dhīrā taranti kavinau puthubuddhināvā||

(kha)

vicittasaṅkhāraparikkhitaṃ imaṃ,

ākhyātasaddaṃ vipulaṃ asaisatau|

paṇamya sambuddhamanantagaucaraṃ,

sugaucaraṃ yaṃ vadatau suṇātha mai||

(ga)

adhikārai maṅgalai caiva, nipphan‍nai cāvadhāraṇai|

anantarai ca pādānai, athasaddau pavattati||

416, 429. atha pubbāni vibhattīnaṃ cha parassapadāni|

atha sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ yāni yāni pubbakāni cha padāni, tāni tāni parassapadasañ‍ñāni haunti|

taṃ yathā? ti anti, si tha, mi ma|

parassapadamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? kattari parassapadaṃ|

407, 439. parāṇyattanaupadāni|

sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ yāni yāni parāni cha padāni| tāni tāni attanaupadasañ‍ñāni haunti|

taṃ yathā? tai antai, sai vhai, ai mhai|

attanaupadamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? attanaupadāni bhāvai ca kammani|

408, 431. dvai dvai paṭhama majjhimuttamapurisā|

tāsaṃ sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ parassapadānaṃ, attanaupadānañ‍ca dvai dvai padāni paṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisasañ‍ñāni haunti|

taṃ yathā? ti anti iti paṭhamapurisā, si tha iti majjhimapurisā, mi ma iti uttamapurisā| attanaupadānampi tai antai iti paṭhamapurisā, sai vhai iti majjhimapurisā, ai mhai iti uttamapurisā| aivaṃ sabbattha|

paṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisamic‍canaina k‍vatthau? nāmamhi payuj‍jamānaipi tulyādhikaraṇai paṭhamau, tumhai majjhimau, amhai uttamau|

409, 441. sabbaisamaikābhidhānai parau purisau|

sabbaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ paṭhamamajjhimuttama purisānaṃ aikābhidhānai parau purisau gahaitabbau|

sau  ca paṭhati, tvañ‍ca paṭhasi, tumhai paṭhatha| sau ca pacati, tvañ‍ca pacasi| tumhai pacatha| aivaṃ saisāsu vibhattīsu parau purisau yaujaitabbau|

410, 432. nāmamhi payuj‍jamānaipi tulyādhikaraṇai paṭhamau|

nāmamhi payuj‍jamānaipi appayuj‍jamānaipi tulyādhikaraṇai paṭhamapurisau hauti|

sau gacchati, tai gacchanti|

appayuj‍jamānaipi – gacchati, gacchanti|

tulyādhikaraṇaiti kimatthaṃ? taina hañ‍ñasai tvaṃ daivadattaina|

411, 436. tumhai majjhimau|

tumhai payuj‍jamānaipi appayuj‍jamānaipi tulyādhikaraṇai majjhimapurisau hauti|

tvaṃ yāsi, tumhai yātha|

appayuj‍jamānaipi – yāsi, yātha|

tulyādhikaraṇaiti kimatthaṃ? tayā pac‍catai audanau|

412, 437. amhai  uttamau|

amhai payuj‍jamānaipi appayuj‍jamānaipi tulyādhikaraṇai uttamapurisau hauti|

ahaṃ yajāmi, mayaṃ yajāma|

appayuj‍jamānaipi – yajāmi, yajāma|

tulyādhikaraṇaiti kimatthaṃ? mayā ij‍jatai buddhau|

413, 427. kālai|

‘‘kālai’’ ic‍caitaṃ adhikāratthaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

414, 428. vattamānā pac‍cuppan‍nai|

pac‍cuppan‍nai kālai vattamānāvibhatti hauti|

pāṭaliputtaṃ gacchati, sāvatthiṃ pavisati|

415, 451. āṇatyā siṭṭhai’nuttakālai pañ‍camī|

āṇatyatthai ca āsīsatthai ca anuttakālai pañ‍camī vibhatti hauti|

karautu kusalaṃ, sukhaṃ tai hautu|

416, 454. anumatiparikappatthaisu sattamī|

anumatyatthai ca parikappatthai ca anuttakālai sattamī vibhatti hauti|

tvaṃ  gacchaiyyāsi, kimahaṃ karaiyyāmi|

417, 460. apac‍cakkhai paraukkhātītai|

apac‍cakkhai atītai kālai paraukkhāvibhatti hauti|

supinai kilamāha, aivaṃ kila paurāṇāhu|

418, 456. hiyyaupabhuti pac‍cakkhai hiyyattanī|

hiyyaupabhuti atītai kālai pac‍cakkhai vā apac‍cakkhai vā hiyyattanī vibhatti hauti|

sau agamā maggaṃ, tai agamū maggaṃ|

419, 469. samīpai’j‍jatanī|

aj‍jappabhuti atītai kālai pac‍cakkhai vā apac‍cakkhai vā samīpai aj‍jatanīvibhatti hauti|

sau maggaṃ agamī, tai maggaṃ agamuṃ|

420, 471. māyaugai sabbakālai ca|

hiyyattanīaj‍jatanīic‍caitā vibhattiyau yadā māyaugā, tadā sabbakālai ca haunti|

mā gamā, mā vacā, mā gamī, mā vacī|

caggahaṇaina  pañ‍camīvibhattipi hauti| mā gacchāhi|

421, 473. anāgatai bhavissantī|

anāgatai kālai bhavissantī vibhatti hauti|

sau gacchissati, karissati| tai gacchissanti, karissanti|

422, 475. kriyātipan‍nai’tītai kālātipatti|

kriyātipan‍namattai atītai kālai kālātipatti vibhatti hauti|

sau cai taṃ yānaṃ alabhissā, agacchissā| tai cai taṃ yānaṃ alabhissaṃsu, agacchissaṃsu|

423, 426. vattamānā ti anti, si tha, mi ma, tai antai, sai vhai, ai mhai|

vattamānā ic‍caisā sañ‍ñā hauti ti anti, si tha, mi ma, tai antai, sai vhai, ai mhai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

vattamānā ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? vattamānā pac‍cuppan‍nai|

424, 450. pañ‍camī tu antu, hitha, mima, taṃ antaṃ, ssu vhau, ai āmasai|

pañ‍camīic‍caisā  sañ‍ñā hauti tu antu, hi tha, mima, taṃ antaṃ, ssu vhau, ai āmasai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

pañ‍camīic‍canaina k‍vatthau? āṇatyāsiṭṭhai, nuttakālai pañ‍camī|

425, 453. sattamī aiyya aiyyuṃ, aiyyā si aiyyā tha, aiyyāmi aiyyāma, aitha airaṃ, aithau aiyyāvhau, aiyyaṃ aiyyāmhai|

sattamī ic‍caisā sañ‍ñā hauti aiyya aiyyuṃ, aiyyāsi aiyyātha, aiyyāmi aiyyāma, aitha airaṃ, aithau, aiyyāvhau, aiyyaṃ aiyyāmhai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

sattamī ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? anumatiparikappatthaisu sattamī|

426, 459. paraukkhā au aittha, aṃmha, ttharai, tthau vhau, iṃ mhai|

paraukkhā ic‍caisā sañ‍ñā hauti a u, aittha, aṃ mha, ttha rai tthau vhau, iṃ mhai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

paraukkhā ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? apac‍cakkhai paraukkhātītai|

427, 455. hiyyattanī  āū, auttha, aṃmhā, tthatthuṃ, sai vhaṃ, iṃ mhasai|

hiyyattanī ic‍caisā sañ‍ñā hauti ā ū, au ttha, aṃ mhā, ttha tthuṃ, sai vhaṃ, iṃ mhasai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

hiyyattanī ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? hiyyaupabhuti pac‍cakkhai hiyyattanī|

428, 468. aj‍jatanī ī uṃ, au ttha, iṃ mhā, ā ū, sai vhaṃ, aṃ mhai|

aj‍jatanī ic‍caisā sañ‍ñā hauti ī uṃ, au ttha, iṃmhā, ā ū, saivhaṃ, aṃ mhai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

aj‍jatanī ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? samīpaij‍jatanī|

429, 472. bhavissantī ssati ssanti, ssasi ssatha, ssāmi ssāma, ssatai ssantai, ssasai ssavhai, ssaṃ ssāmhai|

bhavissantī ic‍caisā sañ‍ñā hauti ssati ssanti, ssasi ssatha, ssāmi ssāma, ssatai ssantai, ssasai ssavhai, ssaṃ ssāmhai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

bhavissantī ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? anāgatai bhavissantī|

430, 373. kālātipatti  ssā ssaṃsu, ssai ssatha, ssaṃ ssāmhā, ssatha sissu, ssasai ssavhai, ssiṃ ssāmhasai|

kālātipatti ic‍caisā sañ‍ñā hauti ssā ssaṃsu, ssai ssatha, ssaṃ ssāmhā, ssatha ssisu, ssasai ssavhai, ssiṃ ssāmhasai ic‍caitaisaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ padānaṃ|

kālātipatti ic‍canaina k‍vatthau? kriyātipan‍nai’ tītai kālātipatti|

431, 458. hiyyattanī sattamī pañ‍camī vattamānā sabbadhātukaṃ|
